const
  BGW     = 640
  BGH     = 408
  FRW     = 380
  FRSCALE = B / float32(FRW)
  BLW     = 672
  BTNW    = 97
  BTNH    = 58
  TYW     = 410
  TYH     = 84
  SHW     = 205
  SHRH    = 190
  CBW     = 168
  CK      = float32(CBW) / 64.0'f32
  CBOX    = 83.75'f32
  SHLW    = 88
  SHLH    = 92
  PLW     = 88
  PLH     = 92
  PBH     = PCSZ / 100.0'f32
  PSTROKE = 1.5'f32 * PBH / 2.0'f32

var
  bgLayer, frameLayer, boardLayer, boardFlipLayer: Layer
  btnLayer, btnDimLayer, confirmL, muteL: Layer
  trayLayer, sheetLayer: Layer
  checkLayer, ringLayer, promoBtnLayer, promoHoverLayer: Layer
  cornerL: array[4, Layer]
  shadowL: array[6, Layer]
  ebonyGlowL: array[6, Layer]
  pieceL: array[6, array[2, Layer]]
  gFlip* = false

proc bakeBg() =
  let base = [ (0.0'f32, f3(hex(0x18281F))), (0.70'f32, f3(hex(0x0F1814))) ]
  let warmA = [ (0.0'f32, 0.17'f32), (0.62'f32, 0.0'f32) ]
  let blkA  = [ (0.0'f32, 0.55'f32), (0.60'f32, 0.0'f32) ]
  let k = SW / float32(BGW)
  let s1x0 = FX;            let s1y0 = FY + 10.0'f32
  let s1x1 = FX + B;        let s1y1 = FY + B + 10.0'f32
  let s2x0 = FX + 20.0'f32; let s2y0 = FY + 40.0'f32 - 20.0'f32
  let s2x1 = FX + B - 20.0'f32; let s2y1 = FY + B + 40.0'f32 + 20.0'f32
  var buf = newSeq[byte](BGW * BGH * 4)
  for j in 0 ..< BGH:
    for i in 0 ..< BGW:
      let x = (float32(i) + 0.5'f32) * k
      let y = (float32(j) + 0.5'f32) * k
      var c = ramp(linT(x, y, SW, SH, 160.0'f32), base)
      c = over(c, fBlack, rampA(radD(x, y, SW, SH, 900.0'f32, 700.0'f32), blkA))
      c = over(c, f3(hex(0xFFCE82)),
               rampA(radD(x, y, SW * 0.20'f32, SH * 0.08'f32, 1100.0'f32, 760.0'f32), warmA))
      let a1 = 0.45'f32 * cGauss(sdRect(x, y, s1x0, s1y0, s1x1, s1y1), 12.0'f32)
      let a2 = 0.70'f32 * cGauss(sdRect(x, y, s2x0, s2y0, s2x1, s2y1), 35.0'f32)
      c = over(c, fBlack, 1.0'f32 - (1.0'f32 - a1) * (1.0'f32 - a2))
      put(buf, (j * BGW + i) * 4, c)
  bgLayer = newLayer(BGW, BGH)
  bgLayer.write(0, 0, BGW, BGH, buf)

proc bakeFrame() =
  let lac = [ (0.0'f32, f3(hex(0x5C2416))), (0.55'f32, f3(hex(0x3B150D))),
              (1.0'f32, f3(hex(0x220A05))) ]
  let shA = [ (0.0'f32, 0.14'f32), (0.26'f32, 0.0'f32),
              (0.72'f32, 0.0'f32), (1.0'f32, 0.05'f32) ]
  let gW = B * 0.84'f32
  let gH = FR * 0.30'f32
  let rad = FR * 0.32'f32 * FRSCALE
  let k = B / float32(FRW)
  var buf = newSeq[byte](FRW * FRW * 4)
  for j in 0 ..< FRW:
    for i in 0 ..< FRW:
      let lx = (float32(i) + 0.5'f32) * k
      let ly = (float32(j) + 0.5'f32) * k
      var c = ramp(radD(lx, ly, B * 0.30'f32, B * 0.10'f32, B * 1.20'f32, B * 0.90'f32), lac)
      c = over(c, fWhite, rampA(linT(lx, ly, B, B, 172.0'f32), shA))
      let gx = lx - B * 0.08'f32
      let gy = ly - 2.0'f32
      if gx >= 0.0'f32 and gy >= 0.0'f32 and gx < gW and gy < gH:
        c = over(c, f3(hex(0xFFECD2)),
                 0.22'f32 * cl(1.0'f32 - radD(gx, gy, gW * 0.5'f32, gH * 0.5'f32,
                                               gW * 0.5'f32, gH * 0.5'f32),
                                0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))
      if ly < 1.0'f32: c = over(c, f3(hex(0xFFE6C8)), 0.22'f32)
      if ly > B - 9.0'f32:
        c = over(c, fBlack, 0.5'f32 * cGauss(max(0.0'f32, 757.33'f32 - ly), 3.69'f32))
      var a = 255
      if lx < 1.0'f32 or ly < 1.0'f32 or lx > B - 1.0'f32 or ly > B - 1.0'f32:
        c = over(c, fBlack, 0.60'f32)
      let rx = float32(i) + 0.5'f32
      let ry = float32(j) + 0.5'f32
      let qx = max(rad - rx, rx - (float32(FRW) - rad))
      let qy = max(rad - ry, ry - (float32(FRW) - rad))
      if qx > 0.0'f32 and qy > 0.0'f32:
        let dd = sqrt(qx * qx + qy * qy)
        if dd > rad: a = 0
        elif dd > rad - 1.0'f32: a = 255 - rnd32((dd - (rad - 1.0'f32)) * 255.0'f32)
      put(buf, (j * FRW + i) * 4, c, uint8(a))
  frameLayer = newLayer(FRW, FRW)
  frameLayer.write(0, 0, FRW, FRW, buf)

proc bakeBoard() =
  var sqGa = newSeq[float32](64)
  var sqV1 = newSeq[float32](64)
  var sqV2 = newSeq[float32](64)
  var seed = 90210'i64
  for s in 0 .. 63:
    let rnd = block:
      seed = (seed * 16807'i64) mod 2147483647'i64
      float32(seed) / 2147483647.0'f32
    let light = (((s shr 3) + (s and 7)) and 1) == 1
    sqGa[s] = (if light: 88.0'f32 + rnd * 6.0'f32 else: -3.0'f32 + rnd * 6.0'f32)
    let r1 = block:
      seed = (seed * 16807'i64) mod 2147483647'i64
      float32(seed) / 2147483647.0'f32
    sqV1[s] = 0.02'f32 + r1 * 0.07'f32
    let r2 = block:
      seed = (seed * 16807'i64) mod 2147483647'i64
      float32(seed) / 2147483647.0'f32
    sqV2[s] = 0.02'f32 + r2 * 0.08'f32

  let ltA = LT_BASE
  let shA = [ (0.0'f32, fWhite), (0.40'f32, f3(hex(0xFFF4DC))),
              (0.60'f32, fBlack), (1.0'f32, fBlack) ]
  var buf0 = newSeq[byte](BLW * BLW * 4)
  var buf1 = newSeq[byte](BLW * BLW * 4)
  for j in 0 ..< BLW:
    for i in 0 ..< BLW:
      let lx = float32(i) + 0.5'f32
      let ly = float32(j) + 0.5'f32
      let col = int32(lx / SQP)
      let row = int32(ly / SQP)
      let baseC = (if ((row + col) and 1) == 0: ltA else: DK_BASE)
      let sh = linT(lx, ly, BW, BW, 135.0'f32)
      for flipPass in 0 .. 1:
        var c = baseC
        let sIdx = if flipPass == 0: (7 - row) * 8 + col else: row * 8 + (7 - col)
        let t = linT(lx - float32(col) * SQP, ly - float32(row) * SQP, SQP, SQP, sqGa[sIdx])
        c = over(c, f3(hex(0xFFF8E6)), sqV1[sIdx] * (1.0'f32 - t))
        c = over(c, f3(hex(0x3C1E0A)), sqV2[sIdx] * t)
        if sh < 0.40'f32:
          c = over(c, shA[1][1], 0.12'f32 * (1.0'f32 - sh / 0.40'f32))
        elif sh > 0.60'f32:
          c = over(c, fBlack, 0.12'f32 * (sh - 0.60'f32) / 0.40'f32)
        if flipPass == 0:
          put(buf0, (j * BLW + i) * 4, c)
        else:
          put(buf1, (j * BLW + i) * 4, c)
  boardLayer = newLayer(BLW, BLW)
  boardLayer.write(0, 0, BLW, BLW, buf0)
  boardFlipLayer = newLayer(BLW, BLW)
  boardFlipLayer.write(0, 0, BLW, BLW, buf1)

proc bakeButtonVariant(layer: var Layer; mode: int) =
  ## mode 0 = normal brass, 1 = disabled dim, 2 = muted saturate(.35) brightness(.86),
  ## mode 3 = `.brass.confirm` coral-brass (`linear-gradient(180deg,#f5b6a0,#d77a5f 50%,#a24d36)`).
  let brassStops = [ (0.0'f32, f3(hex(0xF1D48F))), (0.38'f32, f3(hex(0xD8B265))),
                     (0.62'f32, f3(hex(0xB8914A))), (1.0'f32, f3(hex(0x9A7535))) ]
  let confStops  = [ (0.0'f32, f3(hex(0xF5B6A0))), (0.50'f32, f3(hex(0xD77A5F))),
                     (1.0'f32, f3(hex(0xA24D36))) ]
  let edge = f3(hex(0x6E5125))
  let rad = 8.0'f32
  let w2 = BTNW * 2
  let h2 = BTNH * 2
  var buf = newSeq[byte](w2 * h2 * 4)
  for j in 0 ..< h2:
    let u = (float32(j) + 0.5'f32) / float32(h2)
    let c0 = if mode == 3: ramp(u, confStops) else: ramp(u, brassStops)
    for i in 0 ..< w2:
      var c = c0
      var a = 255
      let px = (float32(i) + 0.5'f32) * 0.5'f32
      let py = (float32(j) + 0.5'f32) * 0.5'f32
      if py < 2.0'f32: c = over(c, f3(hex(0xFFFAE6)), 0.70'f32)
      if py > float32(BTNH) - 2.0'f32: c = over(c, fBlack, 0.25'f32)
      let qx = max(rad - px, px - (float32(BTNW) - rad))
      let qy = max(rad - py, py - (float32(BTNH) - rad))
      if qx > 0.0'f32 and qy > 0.0'f32:
        let dd = sqrt(qx * qx + qy * qy)
        if dd > rad: a = 0
        elif dd > rad - 1.0'f32: a = 255 - rnd32((dd - (rad - 1.0'f32)) * 255.0'f32)
      if a > 0 and (px < 1.0'f32 or px > float32(BTNW) - 1.0'f32 or
                    py < 1.0'f32 or py > float32(BTNH) - 1.0'f32):
        c = edge
      let finalC = if mode == 1: desat3(c)
                   elif mode == 2: satBri3(c, 0.35'f32, 0.86'f32)
                   else: c
      put(buf, (j * w2 + i) * 4, finalC, uint8(a))
  layer = newLayer(w2, h2)
  layer.write(0, 0, w2, h2, buf)

proc bakeButton() =
  bakeButtonVariant(btnLayer, 0)
  bakeButtonVariant(btnDimLayer, 1)

proc bakeExtra() =
  bakeButtonVariant(muteL, 2)
  bakeButtonVariant(confirmL, 3)

  let sz = int(SQS)
  let rCorner = sqrt(42.0'f32 * 42.0'f32 * 2.0'f32)
  var cBuf = newSeq[byte](sz * sz * 4)
  var rBuf = newSeq[byte](sz * sz * 4)
  var pBuf = newSeq[byte](sz * sz * 4)
  var phBuf = newSeq[byte](sz * sz * 4)
  let ckStops = [ (0.0'f32, [255.0'f32, 60.0'f32, 30.0'f32]),
                  (0.32'f32, [230.0'f32, 30.0'f32, 20.0'f32]),
                  (0.72'f32, [200.0'f32, 20.0'f32, 10.0'f32]) ]
  let ckAlpha = [ (0.0'f32, 0.95'f32), (0.32'f32, 0.62'f32), (0.72'f32, 0.0'f32) ]
  let prStops = [ (0.0'f32, f3(hex(0xFFF8E8))), (1.0'f32, f3(hex(0xECDCB8))) ]
  let phStops = [ (0.0'f32, f3(hex(0xFFE7A8))), (1.0'f32, f3(hex(0xE8C77A))) ]
  for j in 0 ..< sz:
    for i in 0 ..< sz:
      let dx = float32(i) + 0.5'f32 - 42.0'f32
      let dy = float32(j) + 0.5'f32 - 42.0'f32
      let u = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) / rCorner
      let ca = rampA(u, ckAlpha)
      put(cBuf, (j * sz + i) * 4, ramp(u, ckStops), uint8(q(rnd32(ca * 255.0'f32))))
      var ra = 0.0'f32
      if u >= 0.60'f32 and u <= 0.76'f32:
        if u < 0.62'f32: ra = 0.32'f32 * (u - 0.60'f32) / 0.02'f32
        elif u <= 0.74'f32: ra = 0.32'f32
        else: ra = 0.32'f32 * (0.76'f32 - u) / 0.02'f32
      put(rBuf, (j * sz + i) * 4, [48.0'f32, 26.0'f32, 10.0'f32], uint8(q(rnd32(ra * 255.0'f32))))
      put(pBuf,  (j * sz + i) * 4, ramp(cl(u, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32), prStops), 255'u8)
      put(phBuf, (j * sz + i) * 4, ramp(cl(u, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32), phStops), 255'u8)
  checkLayer = newLayer(sz, sz)
  checkLayer.write(0, 0, sz, sz, cBuf)
  ringLayer = newLayer(sz, sz)
  ringLayer.write(0, 0, sz, sz, rBuf)
  promoBtnLayer = newLayer(sz, sz)
  promoBtnLayer.write(0, 0, sz, sz, pBuf)
  promoHoverLayer = newLayer(sz, sz)
  promoHoverLayer.write(0, 0, sz, sz, phBuf)

proc bakeTray() =
  let felt = f3(hex(0x1C3328))
  let rad = 10.0'f32
  var buf = newSeq[byte](TYW * TYH * 4)
  for j in 0 ..< TYH:
    for i in 0 ..< TYW:
      let x = float32(i) + 0.5'f32
      let y = float32(j) + 0.5'f32
      let d = radD(x, y, float32(TYW) * 0.30'f32, 0.0'f32,
                   float32(TYW) * 1.20'f32, float32(TYH) * 1.40'f32)
      var c = felt
      if d < 0.60'f32:
        c = over(c, fWhite, 0.05'f32 * (1.0'f32 - d / 0.60'f32))
      let ins = max(0.0'f32, -sdRect(x, y, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32,
                                      float32(TYW), float32(TYH)))
      c = over(c, fBlack, 0.66'f32 * cGauss(ins, 4.0'f32))
      if i == 0 or j == 0 or i == TYW - 1 or j == TYH - 1:
        c = over(c, fBlack, 0.40'f32)
      var a = 255
      let qx = max(rad - x, x - (float32(TYW) - rad))
      let qy = max(rad - y, y - (float32(TYH) - rad))
      if qx > 0.0'f32 and qy > 0.0'f32:
        let dd = sqrt(qx * qx + qy * qy)
        if dd > rad: a = 0
        elif dd > rad - 1.0'f32: a = 255 - rnd32((dd - (rad - 1.0'f32)) * 255.0'f32)
      put(buf, (j * TYW + i) * 4, c, uint8(a))
  trayLayer = newLayer(TYW, TYH)
  trayLayer.write(0, 0, TYW, TYH, buf)

proc bakeSheet() =
  let stops = [ (0.0'f32, f3(hex(0xFBF5E6))), (0.55'f32, f3(hex(0xF3EAD6))),
                (1.0'f32, f3(hex(0xE8DCC0))) ]
  let ink = f3(hex(0xA0783C))
  var buf = newSeq[byte](SHW * SHRH * 4)
  for j in 0 ..< SHRH:
    for i in 0 ..< SHW:
      let d = radD(float32(i), float32(j), float32(SHW) * 0.20'f32, 0.0'f32,
                   float32(SHW) * 1.40'f32, float32(SHRH) * 1.00'f32)
      let edge = min(min(float32(i), float32(SHW - 1 - i)),
                     min(float32(j), float32(SHRH - 1 - j)))
      let a = 0.086'f32 * (if edge < 28.6'f32: 1.0'f32 - edge / 28.6'f32 else: 0.0'f32)
      put(buf, (j * SHW + i) * 4, over(ramp(d, stops), ink, a))
  sheetLayer = newLayer(SHW, SHRH)
  sheetLayer.write(0, 0, SHW, SHRH, buf)

# ───────────────────────────────────────────── brass corners (baked per corner)

proc sdSeg(px, py, ax, ay, bx, by: float32): float32 =
  let vx = bx - ax
  let vy = by - ay
  let wx = px - ax
  let wy = py - ay
  let vv = vx * vx + vy * vy
  var t = 0.0'f32
  if vv > 0.0'f32: t = cl((wx * vx + wy * vy) / vv, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
  let dx = wx - vx * t
  let dy = wy - vy * t
  sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)

proc sdDisc(px, py, cx, cy, r: float32): float32 {.inline.} =
  let dx = px - cx
  let dy = py - cy
  sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) - r

proc plateSD(px, py: float32): float32 =
  var d = sdRect(px, py, 3.0'f32, 3.0'f32, 55.0'f32, 16.0'f32)
  d = min(d, sdRect(px, py, 3.0'f32, 3.0'f32, 16.0'f32, 55.0'f32))
  d = min(d, max(sdDisc(px, py, 55.0'f32, 9.5'f32, 6.5'f32), 55.0'f32 - px))
  d = min(d, max(sdDisc(px, py, 9.5'f32, 55.0'f32, 6.5'f32), 55.0'f32 - py))
  d = min(d, max(sdDisc(px, py, 16.0'f32, 16.0'f32, 9.0'f32),
                 max(16.0'f32 - px, py - 16.0'f32)))
  d

proc boxBlur(src: seq[float32]; w, h, r: int; dst: var seq[float32]) =
  var a = src
  var b = newSeq[float32](w * h)
  for _ in 0 ..< 3:
    for y in 0 ..< h:
      var sum = 0.0'f32
      for x in -r .. r: sum += a[y * w + clampI(x, 0, w - 1)]
      for x in 0 ..< w:
        b[y * w + x] = sum / float32(2 * r + 1)
        sum += a[y * w + clampI(x + r + 1, 0, w - 1)] - a[y * w + clampI(x - r, 0, w - 1)]
    for x in 0 ..< w:
      var sum = 0.0'f32
      for y in -r .. r: sum += b[clampI(y, 0, h - 1) * w + x]
      for y in 0 ..< h:
        a[y * w + x] = sum / float32(2 * r + 1)
        sum += b[clampI(y + r + 1, 0, h - 1) * w + x] - b[clampI(y - r, 0, h - 1) * w + x]
  dst = a

proc bakeCorner(flipX, flipY: bool): Layer =
  let gB = [ (0.0'f32, f3(hex(0xFBE6AE))), (0.35'f32, f3(hex(0xD9B56A))),
             (0.6'f32, f3(hex(0xA98237))), (0.85'f32, f3(hex(0xE2C47E))),
             (1.0'f32, f3(hex(0x8D6A2F))) ]
  let gS = [ (0.0'f32, f3(hex(0xFFF2C9))), (0.5'f32, f3(hex(0xC9A45C))),
             (1.0'f32, f3(hex(0x6E5125))) ]
  let edge = f3(hex(0x4D3713))
  let hi = f3(hex(0xFFF8DC))
  let AA = 0.20'f32
  var mask = newSeq[float32](CBW * CBW)
  var buf = newSeq[byte](CBW * CBW * 4)
  for j in 0 ..< CBW:
    for i in 0 ..< CBW:
      var sx = (float32(i) + 0.5'f32) / CK
      var sy = (float32(j) + 0.5'f32) / CK
      if flipX: sx = 64.0'f32 - sx
      if flipY: sy = 64.0'f32 - sy
      var m = cl(0.5'f32 - plateSD(sx, sy) / AA, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
      for (cx, cy, r, sw) in [(9.6'f32, 9.6'f32, 3.6'f32, 0.7'f32),
                              (47.0'f32, 9.6'f32, 2.6'f32, 0.6'f32),
                              (9.6'f32, 47.0'f32, 2.6'f32, 0.6'f32)]:
        let d = sdDisc(sx, sy, cx, cy, r)
        m = max(m, cl(0.5'f32 - (abs(d) - sw * 0.5'f32) / AA, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))
        m = max(m, cl(0.5'f32 - d / AA, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))
      mask[j * CBW + i] = m
  var sh = newSeq[float32](CBW * CBW)
  boxBlur(mask, CBW, CBW, 8, sh)
  for j in 0 ..< CBW:
    for i in 0 ..< CBW:
      var sx = (float32(i) + 0.5'f32) / CK
      var sy = (float32(j) + 0.5'f32) / CK
      if flipX: sx = 64.0'f32 - sx
      if flipY: sy = 64.0'f32 - sy
      let si = clampI(int(float32(i) - 4.0'f32), 0, CBW - 1)
      let sj = clampI(int(float32(j) - 4.0'f32), 0, CBW - 1)
      let s = 0.50'f32 * sh[sj * CBW + si]
      let p = cl(0.5'f32 - plateSD(sx, sy) / AA, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
      var col: F3 = fBlack
      var a = s
      if p > 0.0'f32:
        let pc = ramp(linT(sx - 3.0'f32, sy - 3.0'f32, 58.5'f32, 58.5'f32, 135.0'f32), gB)
        if p >= s: (col = pc; a = p)
        else: (col = lerp3(fBlack, pc, p / s); a = s)
      let dPlate = plateSD(sx, sy)
      let oc = cl(0.5'f32 - abs(dPlate) / AA, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
      if oc > 0.0'f32:
        col = lerp3(col, edge, oc); a = a + (1.0'f32 - a) * oc
      var dh = sdSeg(sx, sy, 6.0'f32, 6.5'f32, 54.0'f32, 6.5'f32)
      dh = min(dh, sdSeg(sx, sy, 6.5'f32, 6.0'f32, 6.5'f32, 54.0'f32))
      let hc = cl(0.5'f32 - (dh - 0.5'f32) / AA, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32) * 0.65'f32
      if hc > 0.0'f32:
        col = lerp3(col, hi, hc); a = a + (1.0'f32 - a) * hc
      var da = 1.0e9'f32
      for k in 0 .. 8:
        let t0 = float32(k) / 8.0'f32
        let t1 = float32(k + 1) / 8.0'f32
        da = min(da, sdSeg(sx, sy,
          22.0'f32 + 6.0'f32 * t0, 19.0'f32 - 2.0'f32 * t0 - 1.2'f32 * sin(PI32 * t0),
          22.0'f32 + 6.0'f32 * t1, 19.0'f32 - 2.0'f32 * t1 - 1.2'f32 * sin(PI32 * t1)))
      let ac = cl(0.5'f32 - (da - 0.5'f32) / AA, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32) * 0.60'f32
      if ac > 0.0'f32:
        col = lerp3(col, edge, ac); a = a + (1.0'f32 - a) * ac
      for (cx, cy, r, sw, x0, y0, x1, y1, lsw) in
          [(9.6'f32, 9.6'f32, 3.6'f32, 0.7'f32, 7.4'f32, 11.8'f32, 11.8'f32, 7.4'f32, 0.9'f32),
           (47.0'f32, 9.6'f32, 2.6'f32, 0.6'f32, 45.4'f32, 9.6'f32, 48.6'f32, 9.6'f32, 0.8'f32),
           (9.6'f32, 47.0'f32, 2.6'f32, 0.6'f32, 9.6'f32, 45.4'f32, 9.6'f32, 48.6'f32, 0.8'f32)]:
        let d = sdDisc(sx, sy, cx, cy, r)
        if d < 0.0'f32:
          let gr = radD(sx, sy, cx - r + 0.76'f32 * r, cy - r + 0.70'f32 * r, 1.4'f32 * r, 1.4'f32 * r)
          col = lerp3(col, ramp(gr, gS), 1.0'f32)
        let dl = sdSeg(sx, sy, x0, y0, x1, y1)
        let lc = cl(0.5'f32 - (dl - lsw * 0.5'f32) / AA, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
        if lc > 0.0'f32:
          col = lerp3(col, edge, lc); a = a + (1.0'f32 - a) * lc
      put(buf, (j * CBW + i) * 4, col, uint8(q(rnd32(a * 255.0'f32))))
  result = newLayer(CBW, CBW)
  result.write(0, 0, CBW, CBW, buf)

proc bakeCorners() =
  cornerL[0] = bakeCorner(false, false)
  cornerL[1] = bakeCorner(true, false)
  cornerL[2] = bakeCorner(false, true)
  cornerL[3] = bakeCorner(true, true)

# ────────────────────────────────────────── piece gradient & shadow rasteriser

type
  Poly = seq[(float32, float32)]
  Cross = tuple[x: float32, d: int32]

proc pieceIdx(t: char): int {.inline.} =
  case t
  of 'P': 0
  of 'R': 1
  of 'N': 2
  of 'B': 3
  of 'Q': 4
  else:  5

proc piecePaths(t: char): (seq[string], seq[string]) =
  case t
  of 'P': (PP_MAIN, @[])
  of 'R': (PR_MAIN, @[])
  of 'N': (PN_MAIN, @[])
  of 'B': (PB_MAIN, @[])
  of 'Q': (PQ_MAIN, PQ_CIRC)
  else:  (PK_MAIN, @[])

proc pieceLines(t: char): seq[(string, float32, int32)] =
  case t
  of 'P': PP_LINE
  of 'R': PR_LINE
  of 'N': PN_LINE
  of 'B': PB_LINE
  of 'Q': PQ_LINE
  else:  PK_LINE

proc scanNum(s: string; i: var int; v: var float32) =
  ## Pure-float32 SVG path number parser (avoids `float64` `parseFloat` on wasm32).
  let n = s.len
  while i < n and (s[i] == ' ' or s[i] == ',' or s[i] == '\t' or s[i] == '\n'): inc i
  var sign = 1.0'f32
  if i < n and (s[i] == '+' or s[i] == '-'):
    if s[i] == '-': sign = -1.0'f32
    inc i
  var intPart = 0.0'f32
  var hasDigits = false
  while i < n and s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9':
    intPart = intPart * 10.0'f32 + float32(ord(s[i]) - ord('0'))
    hasDigits = true
    inc i
  var fracPart = 0.0'f32
  var divFactor = 1.0'f32
  if i < n and s[i] == '.':
    inc i
    while i < n and s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9':
      fracPart = fracPart * 10.0'f32 + float32(ord(s[i]) - ord('0'))
      divFactor *= 10.0'f32
      hasDigits = true
      inc i
  if hasDigits:
    v = sign * (intPart + fracPart / divFactor)

proc flattenPath(d: string): seq[Poly] =
  var polys: seq[Poly] = @[]
  var cur: Poly = @[]
  var cx = 0.0'f32
  var cy = 0.0'f32
  var i = 0
  var cmd = '\0'

  proc emit(x, y: float32) =
    if cur.len > 0:
      let lx = cur[^1][0]
      let ly = cur[^1][1]
      if abs(lx - x) > 1e-4'f32 or abs(ly - y) > 1e-4'f32: cur.add (x, y)
    else: cur.add (x, y)

  template num(): float32 =
    var v = 0.0'f32
    scanNum(d, i, v)
    v

  while i < d.len:
    let before = i
    if d[i] in {' ', ',', '\t', '\n', '\r'}:
      inc i
    elif d[i] in {'M','L','H','V','C','A','Z','m','l','h','v','c','a','z'}:
      cmd = d[i]
      inc i
      if cmd == 'Z' or cmd == 'z':
        if cur.len > 1: polys.add cur
        if cur.len > 0: cx = cur[0][0]; cy = cur[0][1]
        cur = @[]
        if i == before: inc i
        continue
    if cmd == '\0': break
    if not (d[i] in {'0'..'9', '+', '-', '.'}):
      if i == before: inc i
      continue
    case cmd
    of 'M', 'm':
      let x = num(); let y = num()
      if cmd == 'm': cx += x; cy += y else: cx = x; cy = y
      if cur.len > 1: polys.add cur
      cur = @[(cx, cy)]
      cmd = if cmd == 'M': 'L' else: 'l'
    of 'L', 'l':
      let x = num(); let y = num()
      if cmd == 'l': cx += x; cy += y else: cx = x; cy = y
      emit(cx, cy)
    of 'H', 'h':
      let x = num()
      if cmd == 'h': cx += x else: cx = x
      emit(cx, cy)
    of 'V', 'v':
      let y = num()
      if cmd == 'v': cy += y else: cy = y
      emit(cx, cy)
    of 'C', 'c':
      var a = num(); var b = num(); var e = num(); var f = num()
      var x = num(); var y = num()
      if cmd == 'c':
        a += cx; b += cy; e += cx; f += cy; x += cx; y += cy
      let p0x = cx; let p0y = cy
      for k in 1 .. 20:
        let u = float32(k) / 20.0'f32
        let v = 1.0'f32 - u
        emit(v*v*v*p0x + 3.0'f32*v*v*u*a + 3.0'f32*v*u*u*e + u*u*u*x,
             v*v*v*p0y + 3.0'f32*v*v*u*b + 3.0'f32*v*u*u*f + u*u*u*y)
      cx = x; cy = y
    of 'A', 'a':
      let rx = num(); let ry = num(); let ph = num()
      let fa = num(); let fs = num()
      var x = num(); var y = num()
      if cmd == 'a': x += cx; y += cy
      let phi = ph * 0.017453292'f32
      let cs = cos(phi); let sn = sin(phi)
      let hx = (cx - x) * 0.5'f32; let hy = (cy - y) * 0.5'f32
      var rx2 = max(abs(rx), 1e-4'f32)
      var ry2 = max(abs(ry), 1e-4'f32)
      let lam = (hx*cs + hy*sn) * (hx*cs + hy*sn) / (rx2*rx2) +
                (hy*cs - hx*sn) * (hy*cs - hx*sn) / (ry2*ry2)
      if lam > 1.0'f32:
        let s = sqrt(lam)
        rx2 *= s; ry2 *= s
      let num1 = rx2*rx2*ry2*ry2 - rx2*rx2*hy*hy - ry2*ry2*hx*hx
      let den = rx2*rx2*hy*hy + ry2*ry2*hx*hx
      var co = sqrt(max(0.0'f32, num1 / max(den, 1e-6'f32)))
      if (fa > 0.5'f32) == (fs > 0.5'f32): co = -co
      let cxp = co * rx2 * hy / ry2
      let cyp = -co * ry2 * hx / rx2
      let mxp = (cx + x) * 0.5'f32
      let myp = (cy + y) * 0.5'f32
      let ox = cxp*cs - cyp*sn + mxp
      let oy = cxp*sn + cyp*cs + myp
      let u1x = (cx-ox)*cs + (cy-oy)*sn
      let u1y = -(cx-ox)*sn + (cy-oy)*cs
      let u2x = (x-ox)*cs + (y-oy)*sn
      let u2y = -(x-ox)*sn + (y-oy)*cs
      let th0 = arctan2(u1y, u1x)
      var dth = arctan2(u2y, u2x) - th0
      if fs == 0.0'f32 and dth > 0.0'f32: dth -= 2.0'f32 * PI32
      if fs != 0.0'f32 and dth < 0.0'f32: dth += 2.0'f32 * PI32
      let steps = max(6, int(abs(dth) / 0.035'f32) + 1)
      for k in 1 .. steps:
        let th = th0 + dth * float32(k) / float32(steps)
        emit(ox + rx2*cos(th)*cs - ry2*sin(th)*sn,
             oy + rx2*cos(th)*sn + ry2*sin(th)*cs)
      cx = x; cy = y
    else:
      inc i
    if i == before: inc i
  if cur.len > 1: polys.add cur
  polys

proc sortCross(xs: var seq[Cross]) =
  var i = 1
  while i < xs.len:
    let v = xs[i]
    var j = i - 1
    while j >= 0 and xs[j].x > v.x:
      xs[j + 1] = xs[j]
      dec j
    xs[j + 1] = v
    inc i

proc crossings(p: Poly; y: float32; xs: var seq[Cross]) =
  var k = 0
  let n = p.len
  while k < n:
    let j = (if k + 1 < n: k + 1 else: 0)
    let ay = p[k][1]
    let by = p[j][1]
    if (ay <= y) != (by <= y):
      let ax = p[k][0]
      let bx = p[j][0]
      let t = (y - ay) / (by - ay)
      xs.add ((ax + t * (bx - ax), (if by > ay: 1'i32 else: -1'i32)))
    inc k

proc spanInto(dst: var seq[float32]; row: int; xa, xb: float32; w: int; inv: float32) =
  var a = max(xa, 0.0'f32)
  let b = min(xb, float32(w))
  if not (b > a): return
  var xx = int(floor(a))
  if xx < 0: xx = 0
  let xe = min(int(ceil(b)), w)
  while xx < xe:
    let l = max(a, float32(xx))
    let r = min(b, float32(xx + 1))
    if r > l: dst[row + xx] += (r - l) * inv
    inc xx

proc fillCov(p: Poly; w, h: int; ss = 4): seq[float32] =
  result = newSeq[float32](w * h)
  var xs: seq[Cross] = @[]
  for sy in 0 ..< h * ss:
    let y = (float32(sy) + 0.5'f32) / float32(ss)
    xs.setLen(0)
    crossings(p, y, xs)
    if xs.len < 2: continue
    sortCross(xs)
    let row = (sy div ss) * w
    let inv = 1.0'f32 / float32(ss)
    var wind = 0'i32
    var j = 0
    while j + 1 < xs.len:
      wind += xs[j].d
      if wind != 0: spanInto(result, row, xs[j].x, xs[j + 1].x, w, inv)
      inc j

proc strokeCov(p: Poly; w, h: int; hw: float32; near: seq[byte]): seq[float32] =
  result = newSeq[float32](w * h)
  let n = p.len
  var i = 0
  while i < w * h:
    if near[i] != 0'u8:
      let px = float32(i mod w) + 0.5'f32
      let py = float32(i div w) + 0.5'f32
      var best = 1e9'f32
      var k = 0
      while k < n:
        let j = (if k + 1 < n: k + 1 else: 0)
        let ax = p[k][0]; let ay = p[k][1]
        let bx = p[j][0]; let by = p[j][1]
        let vx = bx - ax; let vy = by - ay
        let vv = vx*vx + vy*vy
        var t = 0.0'f32
        if vv > 0.0'f32: t = cl(((px - ax)*vx + (py - ay)*vy) / vv, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
        let dx = px - (ax + vx*t)
        let dy = py - (ay + vy*t)
        let dd = dx*dx + dy*dy
        if dd < best: best = dd
        inc k
      let dist = sqrt(best)
      let c = hw + 0.5'f32 - dist
      if c > 0.0'f32: result[i] = min(c, 1.0'f32)
    inc i

proc markNear(p: Poly; w, h: int; pad: float32; near: var seq[byte]) =
  var xs: seq[Cross] = @[]
  for sy in 0 ..< h * 4:
    let y = (float32(sy) + 0.5'f32) / 4.0'f32
    xs.setLen(0)
    crossings(p, y, xs)
    for c in xs:
      let a = int(floor(c.x - pad))
      let b = int(ceil(c.x + pad))
      for xx in max(a, 0) ..< min(b, w):
        for yy in max(sy div 4 - 1, 0) ..< min(sy div 4 + 2, h):
          near[yy * w + xx] = 1'u8

proc stampRotEllipse(buf: var seq[byte]; cx, cy, rx, ry, deg: float32; col: F3; alpha: float32) =
  let rad = deg * PI32 / 180.0'f32
  let cs = cos(rad)
  let sn = sin(rad)
  let minR = min(rx, ry)
  for j in 0 ..< PLH:
    let uy = (float32(j) + 0.5'f32) / PBH - cy
    for i in 0 ..< PLW:
      let ux = (float32(i) + 0.5'f32) / PBH - cx
      let ex = (ux * cs + uy * sn) / rx
      let ey = (-ux * sn + uy * cs) / ry
      let d = (sqrt(ex * ex + ey * ey) - 1.0'f32) * minR * PBH
      let cov = cl(0.5'f32 - d, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
      if cov > 0.0'f32:
        blendPut(buf, (j * PLW + i) * 4, col, cov * alpha)

proc bakePieceBody(kind: char; white: bool): Layer =
  let gIv = [ (0.00'f32, f3(hex(0xFFFDF8))), (0.36'f32, f3(hex(0xF7F0E1))),
              (0.74'f32, f3(hex(0xE3D3B3))), (1.00'f32, f3(hex(0xBDA27A))) ]
  let gEb = [ (0.00'f32, f3(hex(0x5D5249))), (0.28'f32, f3(hex(0x2F2A25))),
              (0.70'f32, f3(hex(0x1E1B18))), (1.00'f32, f3(hex(0x0B0A09))) ]
  let stops = (if white: gIv else: gEb)
  let strokeC = (if white: f3(hex(0x6B5638)) else: f3(hex(0x050404)))
  let (main, circ) = piecePaths(kind)
  var buf = newSeq[byte](PLW * PLH * 4)
  for path in main & circ:
    let u = flattenPath(path)
    for q in u:
      var p: Poly = @[]
      for pt in q: p.add ((pt[0] * PBH, pt[1] * PBH))
      if p.len < 3: continue
      var bx0 = 1e9'f32; var by0 = 1e9'f32; var bx1 = -1e9'f32; var by1 = -1e9'f32
      for pt in q:
        bx0 = min(bx0, pt[0]); bx1 = max(bx1, pt[0])
        by0 = min(by0, pt[1]); by1 = max(by1, pt[1])
      let bw = max(bx1 - bx0, 1e-3'f32)
      let bh = max(by1 - by0, 1e-3'f32)
      let fc = fillCov(p, PLW, PLH)
      var near = newSeq[byte](PLW * PLH)
      markNear(p, PLW, PLH, PSTROKE + 1.0'f32, near)
      let sc2 = strokeCov(p, PLW, PLH, PSTROKE, near)
      for i in 0 ..< PLW * PLH:
        let f = cl(fc[i], 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
        let s = cl(sc2[i], 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
        if f <= 0.0'f32 and s <= 0.0'f32: continue
        let ux = cl(((float32(i mod PLW) + 0.5'f32) / PBH - bx0) / bw, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
        let uy = cl(((float32(i div PLW) + 0.5'f32) / PBH - by0) / bh, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
        let t = cl((ux + 0.22'f32 * uy) / 1.0484'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
        let fillC = ramp(t, stops)
        let a = cl(f + s - f * s, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
        let wFill = f * (1.0'f32 - s) / max(a, 1e-5'f32)
        let wStrk = s / max(a, 1e-5'f32)
        let c: F3 = [
          fillC[0] * wFill + strokeC[0] * wStrk,
          fillC[1] * wFill + strokeC[1] * wStrk,
          fillC[2] * wFill + strokeC[2] * wStrk
        ]
        blendPut(buf, i * 4, c, a)

  let hlC: F3  = if white: [255.0'f32, 255.0'f32, 255.0'f32] else: [255.0'f32, 238.0'f32, 214.0'f32]
  let hlA      = if white: 0.85'f32 else: 0.26'f32
  let eyeC: F3 = if white: f3(hex(0x4D3B26)) else: [255.0'f32, 238.0'f32, 214.0'f32]
  let eyeA     = if white: 1.00'f32 else: 0.55'f32
  case kind
  of 'P': stampRotEllipse(buf, 45.0'f32, 41.0'f32, 3.4'f32, 5.4'f32, -28.0'f32, hlC, hlA)
  of 'N': stampRotEllipse(buf, 44.5'f32, 32.5'f32, 2.3'f32, 1.6'f32, -20.0'f32, eyeC, eyeA)
  of 'B': stampRotEllipse(buf, 48.4'f32, 14.6'f32, 1.3'f32, 1.3'f32,   0.0'f32, hlC, hlA)
  of 'Q': stampRotEllipse(buf, 48.6'f32, 22.4'f32, 1.4'f32, 1.4'f32,   0.0'f32, hlC, hlA)
  else: discard

  result = newLayer(PLW, PLH)
  result.write(0, 0, PLW, PLH, buf)

proc bakePieces() =
  const kinds = ['P', 'R', 'N', 'B', 'Q', 'K']
  for i in 0 ..< 6:
    pieceL[i][1] = bakePieceBody(kinds[i], true)
    pieceL[i][0] = bakePieceBody(kinds[i], false)

proc bakePieceShadows() =
  const opaqueBlack = hex(0x000000)
  const warmRim     = hex(0xFFE2B8)
  const kinds = ['P', 'R', 'N', 'B', 'Q', 'K']
  for i in 0 ..< 6:
    let (main, circ) = piecePaths(kinds[i])
    var ld = Draw()
    for path in main & circ:
      ld = ld.svgPath(path, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, PCSZ / 100.0'f32, opaqueBlack, 1.5'f32)
      ld = ld.svgPath(path, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, PCSZ / 100.0'f32, opaqueBlack)
    let l = newLayer(SHLW, SHLH)
    l.draw(ld)
    l.filter(blur, 5.0'f32)
    l.filter(fade, 0.42'f32)
    shadowL[i] = l
  for i in 0 ..< 6:
    let (main, circ) = piecePaths(kinds[i])
    var gd = Draw()
    for path in main & circ:
      gd = gd.svgPath(path, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, PCSZ / 100.0'f32, warmRim, 1.5'f32)
      gd = gd.svgPath(path, 0.0'f32, 0.0'f32, PCSZ / 100.0'f32, warmRim)
    let g = newLayer(SHLW, SHLH)
    g.draw(gd)
    g.filter(blur, 1.4'f32)
    g.filter(fade, 0.35'f32)
    ebonyGlowL[i] = g

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 7. PROCEDURAL AUDIO ENGINE (`nkSynth` WOODEN CLACK & HARMONIC BELLS)
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

type
  ScheduledNote = object
    voiceIdx: int     ## 0 = noise burst, 1 = wood body thump, 2..4 = bell partials
    startAt: float32  ## seconds (nowSec)
    stopAt: float32   ## seconds (nowSec)
    freq: float32
    cutoff: float32
    vol: float32
    started: bool

var
  page*: Node
  scene*: Node
  ready = false
  sceneCmds = ""
  nowSec = 0.0'f32

  soundOn* = true
  synthVoices: array[5, Node]
  synthReady = false
  wavReady = false
  sndMv, sndMvCheck, sndMvMate, sndMvDraw: Sound
  sndCap, sndCapCheck, sndCapMate, sndCapDraw: Sound
  sndCst, sndCstCheck, sndCstMate, sndCstDraw: Sound
  sndTick: Sound
  noteQueue: seq[ScheduledNote] = @[]
  audioRngSeed: uint32 = 0x9E3779B9'u32

proc nextAudioRand(): float32 =
  audioRngSeed = audioRngSeed * 1664525'u32 + 1013904223'u32
  float32(audioRngSeed and 0xFFFF'u32) / 65535.0'f32

proc loadSounds*() =
  if not wavReady:
    sndMv = sound("mv.wav"); sndMvCheck = sound("mv-check.wav")
    sndMvMate = sound("mv-mate.wav"); sndMvDraw = sound("mv-draw.wav")
    sndCap = sound("cap.wav"); sndCapCheck = sound("cap-check.wav")
    sndCapMate = sound("cap-mate.wav"); sndCapDraw = sound("cap-draw.wav")
    sndCst = sound("cst.wav"); sndCstCheck = sound("cst-check.wav")
    sndCstMate = sound("cst-mate.wav"); sndCstDraw = sound("cst-draw.wav")
    sndTick = sound("tick.wav")
    wavReady = true
  if synthReady: return
  for i in 0 .. 4:
    let v = make(nkSynth, page)
    v.set(pPlaying, false)
    if i == 0:
      v.set(pWave, 4'i32)           ## White noise
      v.set(pFilterKind, 3'i32)     ## Band-pass
      v.set(pResonance, 1.6'f32)
      v.set(pAttack, 0.002'f32)
      v.set(pRelease, 0.045'f32)
    elif i == 1:
      v.set(pWave, 0'i32)           ## Sine body thump
      v.set(pFilterKind, 0'i32)
      v.set(pAttack, 0.003'f32)
      v.set(pRelease, 0.055'f32)
    else:
      v.set(pWave, 0'i32)           ## Bell sine partial
      v.set(pFilterKind, 0'i32)
      v.set(pAttack, 0.010'f32)
      v.set(pRelease, 0.45'f32)
    synthVoices[i] = v
  synthReady = true

proc scheduleKnock(delaySec, freq, gain, dur: float32) =
  if not soundOn or not synthReady: return
  let t0 = nowSec + delaySec
  noteQueue.add ScheduledNote(
    voiceIdx: 0, startAt: t0, stopAt: t0 + dur * 0.55'f32,
    freq: freq, cutoff: freq, vol: cl(gain * 0.65'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))
  noteQueue.add ScheduledNote(
    voiceIdx: 1, startAt: t0, stopAt: t0 + dur * 0.70'f32,
    freq: freq * 0.14'f32, cutoff: 0.0'f32, vol: cl(gain * 0.45'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))

proc scheduleBell(delaySec, baseFreq, gain, dur: float32) =
  if not soundOn or not synthReady: return
  const partials = [1.0'f32, 2.76'f32, 5.4'f32]
  let t0 = nowSec + delaySec
  for i in 0 .. 2:
    let k = float32(i + 1)
    noteQueue.add ScheduledNote(
      voiceIdx: 2 + i, startAt: t0, stopAt: t0 + (dur / k) * 0.45'f32,
      freq: baseFreq * partials[i], cutoff: 0.0'f32,
      vol: cl(gain / k, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))

proc soundTick*() =
  scheduleKnock(0.005'f32, 2600.0'f32, 0.12'f32, 0.04'f32)
  if soundOn and wavReady: play(sndTick)

proc soundMove*(kind: string; check: bool; ending: EndState) =
  if not soundOn: return
  if kind == "capture":
    scheduleKnock(0.010'f32, 1250.0'f32, 0.70'f32, 0.12'f32)
    scheduleKnock(0.055'f32, 1900.0'f32, 0.35'f32, 0.08'f32)
  elif kind == "castle":
    scheduleKnock(0.010'f32, 1700.0'f32, 0.50'f32, 0.09'f32)
    scheduleKnock(0.120'f32, 1500.0'f32, 0.45'f32, 0.09'f32)
  else:
    scheduleKnock(0.010'f32, 1650.0'f32 + nextAudioRand() * 200.0'f32, 0.55'f32, 0.09'f32)

  if ending.isOver and ending.kind == "mate":
    scheduleBell(0.130'f32, 392.0'f32, 0.12'f32, 2.2'f32)
  elif ending.isOver:
    scheduleBell(0.130'f32, 523.25'f32, 0.08'f32, 1.6'f32)
  elif check:
    scheduleBell(0.090'f32, 1046.5'f32, 0.05'f32, 0.7'f32)

  if wavReady:
    let endIdx = if ending.isOver and ending.kind == "mate": 2
                 elif ending.isOver: 3
                 elif check: 1 else: 0
    if kind == "capture":
      play((if endIdx == 2: sndCapMate elif endIdx == 3: sndCapDraw
            elif endIdx == 1: sndCapCheck else: sndCap))
    elif kind == "castle":
      play((if endIdx == 2: sndCstMate elif endIdx == 3: sndCstDraw
            elif endIdx == 1: sndCstCheck else: sndCst))
    else:
      play((if endIdx == 2: sndMvMate elif endIdx == 3: sndMvDraw
            elif endIdx == 1: sndMvCheck else: sndMv))

proc stepAudio(): bool =
  if noteQueue.len == 0: return false
  var i = 0
  while i < noteQueue.len:
    if not noteQueue[i].started and nowSec >= noteQueue[i].startAt:
      let v = synthVoices[noteQueue[i].voiceIdx]
      if noteQueue[i].voiceIdx == 0:
        v.set(pCutoff, noteQueue[i].cutoff)
      else:
        v.set(pFreq, noteQueue[i].freq)
      v.set(pVolume, noteQueue[i].vol)
      v.set(pPlaying, true)
      noteQueue[i].started = true
    if noteQueue[i].started and nowSec >= noteQueue[i].stopAt:
      synthVoices[noteQueue[i].voiceIdx].set(pPlaying, false)
      noteQueue.delete(i)
    else:
      inc i
  noteQueue.len > 0

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 8. INTERACTIVE GAME STATE, ANIMATIONS & CONTROLLERS
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

type
  Glider = object
    piece: ChessPiece
    x0, y0, x1, y1: float32
    t0, dur: float32
    dropped: bool

  FadingPiece = object
    piece: ChessPiece
    sq: int32
    t0: float32

  PromoState = object
    active: bool
    move: Move
    dropped: bool
    hoverIdx: int
    focusIdx: int

var
  gGame* = newGame()
  gNumber* = 1
  selSq* = -1'i32
  focusSq* = 12'i32
  kbFocusVisible = false
  hoverSq = -1'i32

  hovBtn* = -1
  pressBtn* = -1
  confirmT0* = -1.0'f32

  appearT0* = -10.0'f32
  flipT0 = -1.0'f32
  flipSwapped = false
  gliders: seq[Glider] = @[]
  fadingPieces: seq[FadingPiece] = @[]

  dragActive = false
  dragArmed = false
  dragSq = -1'i32
  dragPiece: ChessPiece
  dragWasSelected = false
  dragStartX = 0.0'f32
  dragStartY = 0.0'f32
  dragCurX = 0.0'f32
  dragCurY = 0.0'f32

  promoPending: PromoState

  nameWStr = ""
  nameBStr = ""
  activeInput = 0
  movesScrollRow = 0

proc invalidateScene() {.inline.} =
  sceneCmds = ""

proc repaint*()

proc dispRowCol*(s: int32): (int32, int32) {.inline.} =
  if gFlip: ((s shr 3), 7'i32 - (s and 7))
  else: (7'i32 - (s shr 3), s and 7)

proc fromDisp*(row, col: int32): int32 {.inline.} =
  if gFlip: row * 8'i32 + (7'i32 - col)
  else: (7'i32 - row) * 8'i32 + col

proc sqScreenPos(s: int32): (float32, float32) {.inline.} =
  let (r, c) = dispRowCol(s)
  (BX + float32(c) * SQP, BY + float32(r) * SQP)

proc syncPieces*() =
  invalidateScene()

proc refreshLegal*() =
  gGame.legalMoves = legal(gGame.st)

proc playerName(c: char): string =
  if c == 'w':
    let s = nameWStr.strip()
    if s.len > 0: s else: "White"
  else:
    let s = nameBStr.strip()
    if s.len > 0: s else: "Black"

proc sideToMove*(): char {.inline.} = gGame.st.turn

proc statusMode*(): int =
  if gGame.ending.isOver: 2
  elif inCheck(gGame.st): 1
  else: 0

proc statusText*(): string =
  if gGame.ending.isOver:
    case gGame.ending.kind
    of "mate":       "Checkmate \u2014 " & playerName(gGame.ending.winner) & " wins"
    of "stalemate":  "Stalemate \u2014 the game is drawn"
    of "fifty":      "Drawn by the fifty-move rule"
    of "material":   "Drawn \u2014 insufficient material"
    of "repetition": "Drawn by threefold repetition"
    else:            "Game drawn"
  else:
    if inCheck(gGame.st):
      playerName(gGame.st.turn) & " is in check"
    else:
      playerName(gGame.st.turn) & " to move"

proc evaluateEnd*() =
  let st = gGame.st
  if gGame.legalMoves.len == 0:
    if inCheck(st):
      gGame.ending = EndState(isOver: true, kind: "mate", winner: otherSide(st.turn))
    else:
      gGame.ending = EndState(isOver: true, kind: "stalemate", winner: '\0')
    return
  if st.half >= 100:
    gGame.ending = EndState(isOver: true, kind: "fifty", winner: '\0')
    return
  if insufficient(st.board):
    gGame.ending = EndState(isOver: true, kind: "material", winner: '\0')
    return
  let key = posKey(st)
  var n = if key == gGame.initKey: 1 else: 0
  for h in gGame.hist:
    if h.key == key: inc n
  if n >= 3:
    gGame.ending = EndState(isOver: true, kind: "repetition", winner: '\0')
    return
  gGame.ending = EndState(isOver: false, kind: "", winner: '\0')

proc trayLists*(): (seq[char], seq[char], int, int) =
  var byW: seq[char] = @[]
  var byB: seq[char] = @[]
  for h in gGame.hist:
    if not h.cap.isEmpty:
      if h.cap.c == 'w': byB.add h.cap.t
      else:              byW.add h.cap.t
  proc sortOrder(t: char): int =
    -pieceVal(t) * 10 - (if t == 'B': 1 else: 0)
  proc sortPieces(xs: var seq[char]) =
    var i = 1
    while i < xs.len:
      let v = xs[i]
      var j = i - 1
      while j >= 0 and sortOrder(xs[j]) > sortOrder(v):
        xs[j + 1] = xs[j]
        dec j
      xs[j + 1] = v
      inc i
  sortPieces(byW)
  sortPieces(byB)
  var mw = 0
  var mb = 0
  for i in 0 .. 63:
    let p = gGame.st.board[i]
    if not p.isEmpty:
      if p.c == 'w': mw += pieceVal(p.t)
      else:          mb += pieceVal(p.t)
  let advW = if mw > mb: mw - mb else: 0
  let advB = if mb > mw: mb - mw else: 0
  (byW, byB, advW, advB)

proc scrollMovesToEnd() =
  let totalRows = (gGame.hist.len + 1) div 2
  const maxVisibleRows = 9
  movesScrollRow = max(0, totalRows - maxVisibleRows)

proc commitMove*(m: Move; dropped: bool) =
  let before = gGame.st
  let san = toSAN(before, m, gGame.legalMoves)
  let nxt = applyMove(before, m)
  let mover = before.board[m.src]

  if not nxt.captured.isEmpty:
    let capSq = if m.flag == "ep": m.dst + (if mover.c == 'w': -8'i32 else: 8'i32) else: m.dst
    fadingPieces.add FadingPiece(piece: nxt.captured, sq: capSq, t0: nowSec)

  let (x0, y0) = if dropped and dragActive: (dragCurX - PCSZ * 0.5'f32, dragCurY - PCSZ * 0.5'f32)
                 else: sqScreenPos(m.src)
  let (x1, y1) = sqScreenPos(m.dst)
  let movedPiece = nxt.board[m.dst]
  gliders.add Glider(
    piece: movedPiece, x0: x0, y0: y0, x1: x1, y1: y1,
    t0: nowSec, dur: (if dropped: 0.12'f32 else: 0.34'f32), dropped: dropped)

  if m.flag == "castleK" or m.flag == "castleQ":
    let rFrom = if m.flag == "castleK": m.dst + 1'i32 else: m.dst - 2'i32
    let rTo   = if m.flag == "castleK": m.dst - 1'i32 else: m.dst + 1'i32
    let (rx0, ry0) = sqScreenPos(rFrom)
    let (rx1, ry1) = sqScreenPos(rTo)
    gliders.add Glider(
      piece: nxt.board[rTo], x0: rx0, y0: ry0, x1: rx1, y1: ry1,
      t0: nowSec, dur: 0.42'f32, dropped: false)

  var cleanNext = nxt
  cleanNext.captured = ChessPiece()
  let key = posKey(cleanNext)
  gGame.hist.add HistEntry(prev: before, move: m, san: san, cap: nxt.captured, key: key)
  gGame.st = cleanNext
  refreshLegal()
  selSq = -1
  evaluateEnd()
  scrollMovesToEnd()

  let sndKind = if (not nxt.captured.isEmpty) or m.flag == "ep": "capture"
                elif m.flag.startsWith("castle"): "castle"
                else: "move"
  soundMove(sndKind, inCheck(cleanNext), gGame.ending)
  invalidateScene()

proc openPromo*(m: Move; dropped: bool) =
  promoPending = PromoState(
    active: true, move: m, dropped: dropped, hoverIdx: -1,
    focusIdx: (if dispRowCol(m.dst)[0] == 0: 0 else: 4))
  invalidateScene()

proc closePromo*(choice: char) =
  if not promoPending.active: return
  let m = promoPending.move
  let dropped = promoPending.dropped
  promoPending.active = false
  if choice in {'Q', 'R', 'B', 'N'}:
    var pm = m
    pm.promo = choice
    commitMove(pm, dropped)
  else:
    selSq = -1
    invalidateScene()

proc tryMove*(src, dst: int32; dropped: bool): bool =
  for m in gGame.legalMoves:
    if m.src == src and m.dst == dst:
      if m.flag == "promo":
        openPromo(m, dropped)
      else:
        commitMove(m, dropped)
      return true
  false

proc doUndo*() =
  if promoPending.active:
    closePromo('\0')
    repaint()
    return
  if gGame.hist.len == 0: return
  let h = gGame.hist[^1]
  gGame.hist.setLen(gGame.hist.len - 1)
  let mover = h.prev.board[h.move.src]
  let (x0, y0) = sqScreenPos(h.move.dst)
  let (x1, y1) = sqScreenPos(h.move.src)
  gliders.add Glider(
    piece: mover, x0: x0, y0: y0, x1: x1, y1: y1,
    t0: nowSec, dur: 0.12'f32, dropped: false)
  if h.move.flag == "castleK" or h.move.flag == "castleQ":
    let rFrom = if h.move.flag == "castleK": h.move.dst - 1'i32 else: h.move.dst + 1'i32
    let rTo   = if h.move.flag == "castleK": h.move.dst + 1'i32 else: h.move.dst - 2'i32
    let (rx0, ry0) = sqScreenPos(rFrom)
    let (rx1, ry1) = sqScreenPos(rTo)
    gliders.add Glider(
      piece: h.prev.board[rTo], x0: rx0, y0: ry0, x1: rx1, y1: ry1,
      t0: nowSec, dur: 0.34'f32, dropped: false)
  gGame.st = h.prev
  refreshLegal()
  evaluateEnd()
  selSq = -1
  scrollMovesToEnd()
  soundTick()
  invalidateScene()
  repaint()

proc doFlip*() =
  if promoPending.active:
    closePromo('\0')
  flipT0 = nowSec
  flipSwapped = false
  invalidateScene()
  repaint()

proc doNewGame*() =
  if gGame.hist.len > 0 and (not gGame.ending.isOver) and confirmT0 < 0.0'f32:
    confirmT0 = nowSec
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()
    return
  confirmT0 = -1.0'f32
  if promoPending.active:
    promoPending.active = false
  gliders.setLen(0)
  fadingPieces.setLen(0)
  gGame = newGame()
  inc gNumber
  selSq = -1
  movesScrollRow = 0
  appearT0 = nowSec
  invalidateScene()
  repaint()

proc fadeK*(): float32 =
  if flipT0 < 0.0'f32: return 1.0'f32
  let dt = nowSec - flipT0
  if dt < 0.19'f32:
    return cl(1.0'f32 - dt / 0.19'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
  elif dt < 0.37'f32:
    return cl((dt - 0.19'f32) / 0.18'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
  else:
    return 1.0'f32

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 9. DYNAMIC BOARD, PIECE, PROMO, TRAY & SCORE SHEET RENDERING
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

proc drawPieceCustom(d: var Draw; x, y, scaleFactor: float32; p: ChessPiece;
                     shadowDy = 3.0'f32; opacity = 1.0'f32;
                     forceSize = false) =
  if p.isEmpty or opacity <= 0.001'f32: return
  let si = pieceIdx(p.t)
  let isW = if p.c == 'w': 1 else: 0
  let sc = (PCSZ * scaleFactor) / 100.0'f32
  let lw = float32(PLW) * scaleFactor
  let lh = float32(PLH) * scaleFactor

  let hl  = if p.c == 'w': argb(255, 255, 255, 0.85'f32) else: argb(255, 238, 214, 0.26'f32)
  let ln  = if p.c == 'w': argb(107, 86, 56, 0.55'f32)   else: argb(255, 238, 214, 0.20'f32)
  let eye = if p.c == 'w': hex(0x4D3B26)                 else: argb(255, 238, 214, 0.55'f32)
  let lines = pieceLines(p.t)

  let useAlpha = opacity < 0.999'f32
  if useAlpha:
    d = d.save().alpha(opacity)

  if abs(scaleFactor - 1.0'f32) < 1e-4'f32 and not forceSize:
    d = d.layer(shadowL[si], x, y + shadowDy)
    if p.c == 'b':
      d = d.layer(ebonyGlowL[si], x, y)
    d = d.layer(pieceL[si][isW], x, y)
    for (path, wdt, kind) in lines:
      let col = if kind == 1: hl elif kind == 2: ln else: eye
      d = d.svgPath(path, x, y, sc, col, wdt)
  else:
    d = d.layer(shadowL[si], x, y + shadowDy * scaleFactor, lw, lh)
    if p.c == 'b':
      d = d.layer(ebonyGlowL[si], x, y, lw, lh)
    d = d.layer(pieceL[si][isW], x, y, lw, lh)
    for (path, wdt, kind) in lines:
      let col = if kind == 1: hl elif kind == 2: ln else: eye
      d = d.svgPath(path, x, y, sc, col, wdt * scaleFactor)

  if useAlpha:
    d = d.restore()

proc isPieceGliding(id: int32): bool =
  for g in gliders:
    if g.piece.id == id: return true
  false

proc drawHi*(d: var Draw) =
  let b = gGame.st.board
  let hasLast = gGame.hist.len > 0
  let lastFrom = if hasLast: gGame.hist[^1].move.src else: -1'i32
  let lastTo   = if hasLast: gGame.hist[^1].move.dst else: -1'i32
  let ckSq = if inCheck(gGame.st): kingSq(b, gGame.st.turn) else: -1'i32

  for s in 0'i32 .. 63'i32:
    let (x0, y0) = sqScreenPos(s)
    let isLight = (((s shr 3) + (s and 7)) and 1) == 1
    if s == lastFrom or s == lastTo:
      let col = if isLight: argb(222, 176, 64, 0.36'f32) else: argb(214, 160, 52, 0.42'f32)
      d = d.rect(x0, y0, SQS, SQS, col)
    if s == selSq:
      d = d.rect(x0, y0, SQS, SQS, argb(255, 216, 120, 0.50'f32))
      let borderCol = argb(255, 238, 190, 0.70'f32)
      d = d.rect(x0, y0, SQS, 3.0'f32, borderCol)
      d = d.rect(x0, y0 + SQS - 3.0'f32, SQS, 3.0'f32, borderCol)
      d = d.rect(x0, y0 + 3.0'f32, 3.0'f32, SQS - 6.0'f32, borderCol)
      d = d.rect(x0 + SQS - 3.0'f32, y0 + 3.0'f32, 3.0'f32, SQS - 6.0'f32, borderCol)
    if s == ckSq:
      d = d.layer(checkLayer, x0, y0, SQS, SQS)
    if kbFocusVisible and s == focusSq:
      let blk = argb(0, 0, 0, 0.35'f32)
      d = d.rect(x0, y0, SQS, 5.0'f32, blk)
      d = d.rect(x0, y0 + SQS - 5.0'f32, SQS, 5.0'f32, blk)
      d = d.rect(x0, y0 + 5.0'f32, 5.0'f32, SQS - 10.0'f32, blk)
      d = d.rect(x0 + SQS - 5.0'f32, y0 + 5.0'f32, 5.0'f32, SQS - 10.0'f32, blk)
      let fCol = hex(0xF5D68A)
      d = d.rect(x0, y0, SQS, 3.0'f32, fCol)
      d = d.rect(x0, y0 + SQS - 3.0'f32, SQS, 3.0'f32, fCol)
      d = d.rect(x0, y0 + 3.0'f32, 3.0'f32, SQS - 6.0'f32, fCol)
      d = d.rect(x0 + SQS - 3.0'f32, y0 + 3.0'f32, 3.0'f32, SQS - 6.0'f32, fCol)

  if selSq >= 0:
    for m in gGame.legalMoves:
      if m.src == selSq:
        let (x0, y0) = sqScreenPos(m.dst)
        let isCap = (not b[m.dst].isEmpty) or m.flag == "ep"
        if isCap:
          d = d.layer(ringLayer, x0, y0, SQS, SQS)
        else:
          let alpha = if hoverSq == m.dst: 0.45'f32 else: 0.30'f32
          d = d.circle(x0 + SQS * 0.5'f32, y0 + SQS * 0.5'f32, SQS * 0.13'f32,
                       argb(48, 26, 10, alpha))

proc drawPiecesAnim*(d: var Draw) =
  for fp in fadingPieces:
    let dt = nowSec - fp.t0
    if dt >= 0.0'f32 and dt < 0.36'f32:
      let op = cl(1.0'f32 - dt / 0.30'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
      let k = cubicBezier(0.3'f32, 1.4'f32, 0.5'f32, 1.0'f32, cl(dt / 0.22'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32))
      let sc = 1.0'f32 - 0.28'f32 * k
      let (x0, y0) = sqScreenPos(fp.sq)
      let pad = PCSZ * (1.0'f32 - sc) * 0.5'f32
      drawPieceCustom(d, x0 + pad, y0 + pad, sc, fp.piece, 3.0'f32, op)

  let b = gGame.st.board
  for s in 0'i32 .. 63'i32:
    var p = b[s]
    var drawSq = s
    if promoPending.active:
      if s == promoPending.move.src: continue
      if s == promoPending.move.dst:
        p = b[promoPending.move.src]
        drawSq = promoPending.move.dst
    if p.isEmpty: continue
    if dragActive and s == dragSq: continue
    if isPieceGliding(p.id): continue

    let (x0, y0) = sqScreenPos(drawSq)
    var dx = x0
    var dy = y0
    var sc = 1.0'f32
    var op = 1.0'f32
    var shDy = 3.0'f32

    if appearT0 >= 0.0'f32:
      let rankDist = if p.c == 'w': int(s shr 3) else: 7 - int(s shr 3)
      let delay = float32(abs(rankDist)) * 0.07'f32 + float32(s and 7) * 0.025'f32
      let elapsed = nowSec - appearT0 - delay
      if elapsed <= 0.0'f32:
        continue
      elif elapsed < 0.55'f32:
        let u = cl(elapsed / 0.55'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
        let k = cubicBezier(0.3'f32, 1.3'f32, 0.5'f32, 1.0'f32, u)
        op = cl(u * 1.6'f32, 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
        sc = 0.90'f32 + 0.10'f32 * k
        let pad = PCSZ * (1.0'f32 - sc) * 0.5'f32
        dx = x0 + pad
        dy = y0 + pad - PCSZ * 0.22'f32 * (1.0'f32 - k)

    if drawSq == selSq and (not dragActive):
      sc = 1.06'f32
      let pad = PCSZ * (1.0'f32 - sc) * 0.5'f32
      dx = x0 + pad
      dy = y0 + pad - PCSZ * 0.05'f32
      shDy = 9.0'f32

    let isGhost = promoPending.active and drawSq == promoPending.move.dst
    drawPieceCustom(d, dx, dy, sc, p, shDy, op, isGhost)

proc drawGliders*(d: var Draw) =
  for g in gliders:
    let u = cl((nowSec - g.t0) / max(g.dur, 0.001'f32), 0.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
    let k = if g.dropped: 1.0'f32 - (1.0'f32 - u) * (1.0'f32 - u)
            else: cubicBezier(0.25'f32, 0.8'f32, 0.25'f32, 1.0'f32, u)
    let gx = g.x0 + (g.x1 - g.x0) * k
    let gy = g.y0 + (g.y1 - g.y0) * k
    drawPieceCustom(d, gx, gy, 1.0'f32, g.piece, 5.0'f32, 1.0'f32)

  if dragActive and not dragPiece.isEmpty:
    let sc = 1.14'f32
    let sz = PCSZ * sc
    drawPieceCustom(d, dragCurX - sz * 0.5'f32, dragCurY - sz * 0.5'f32, sc, dragPiece, 14.0'f32, 1.0'f32)

proc promoBoxGeom(): (float32, float32, float32, float32, bool) =
  let (row, col) = dispRowCol(promoPending.move.dst)
  let fromTop = row == 0
  let w = SQS
  let cancelH = 26.0'f32
  let h = 4.0'f32 * SQS + cancelH
  let x = BX + float32(col) * SQP
  let y = if fromTop: BY else: BY + BW - h
  (x, y, w, h, fromTop)

proc drawPromo*(d: var Draw) =
  if not promoPending.active: return
  let (x, y, w, h, fromTop) = promoBoxGeom()
  let c = gGame.st.turn
  const cancelH = 26.0'f32

  d = d.rect(x - 4.0'f32, y + 10.0'f32, w + 8.0'f32, h + 12.0'f32, argb(0, 0, 0, 0.42'f32), 10.0'f32)
  d = d.rect(x - 2.0'f32, y - 2.0'f32, w + 4.0'f32, h + 4.0'f32, hex(0xB8914A), 8.0'f32)
  d = d.rect(x, y, w, h, hex(0xF7ECD4), 6.0'f32)

  let types = if fromTop: ['Q', 'R', 'B', 'N'] else: ['N', 'B', 'R', 'Q']
  var cy = y

  if not fromTop:
    let isHov = promoPending.hoverIdx == 0 or promoPending.focusIdx == 0
    d = d.rect(x, cy, w, cancelH, (if isHov: hex(0x5A2216) else: hex(0x3B150D)))
    centred(d, x + w * 0.5'f32, cy + 5.0'f32, 12.0'f32, hex(0xE8CF97), "CANCEL", 12.0'f32 * 0.14'f32)
    cy += cancelH

  for i in 0 .. 3:
    let slotIdx = if fromTop: i else: i + 1
    let isHov = promoPending.hoverIdx == slotIdx or promoPending.focusIdx == slotIdx
    d = d.layer((if isHov: promoHoverLayer else: promoBtnLayer), x, cy)
    let heatCy = if fromTop and slotIdx == 0: cy + 43.0'f32
                 elif (not fromTop) and slotIdx == 4: cy + 41.0'f32
                 else: cy + 42.0'f32
    d = d.circle(x + w * 0.5'f32, heatCy, 30.0'f32,
                 (if isHov: hex(0xF7E3AE) else: hex(0xF4EAD0)))
    d = d.circle(x + w * 0.5'f32, heatCy, 15.0'f32,
                 (if isHov: hex(0xFFE7A8) else: hex(0xFFF8E8)))
    let pad = SQS * 0.06'f32
    drawPieceCustom(d, x + pad, cy + pad, 0.88'f32, ChessPiece(t: types[i], c: c, id: 0), 2.5'f32, 1.0'f32)
    cy += SQS

  if fromTop:
    let isHov = promoPending.hoverIdx == 4 or promoPending.focusIdx == 4
    d = d.rect(x, cy, w, cancelH, (if isHov: hex(0x5A2216) else: hex(0x3B150D)))
    centred(d, x + w * 0.5'f32, cy + 5.0'f32, 12.0'f32, hex(0xE8CF97), "CANCEL", 12.0'f32 * 0.14'f32)

proc drawCapsRow*(d: var Draw; r: int; caps: seq[char]; color: char) =
  let cy = TRY + 20.0'f32 + float32(r) * 41.0'f32
  var cx = PX + 116.0'f32
  var prev = '\0'
  let sc = 24.0'f32 / PCSZ
  for t in caps:
    if prev != '\0' and prev != t:
      cx += 12.0'f32
    drawPieceCustom(d, cx, cy, sc, ChessPiece(t: t, c: color, id: 0), 1.0'f32, 1.0'f32)
    cx += 17.0'f32
    prev = t

proc drawNames*(d: var Draw) =
  let py = SHY + 35.0'f32
  let ph = argb(117, 117, 117, 0.62'f32)
  for field in 0 .. 1:
    let tx = if field == 0: PX + 102.0'f32 else: PX + 284.0'f32
    let rx = if field == 0: PX + 99.0'f32 else: PX + 281.0'f32
    let rw = if field == 0: 120.0'f32 else: PX + PW - 16.0'f32 - (PX + 281.0'f32)
    let val = if field == 0: nameWStr else: nameBStr
    let focused = (field == 0 and activeInput == 1) or (field == 1 and activeInput == 2)
    if focused:
      d = d.rect(rx, py + 2.0'f32, rw, 25.0'f32, argb(29, 58, 107, 0.05'f32))
    if val.len == 0 and not focused:
      d = d.tFace(tx, py + 6.0'f32, 16.0'f32, ph, SERIF_FONT,
                  (if field == 0: "Ivory player" else: "Ebony player"))
    elif val.len > 0:
      d = d.tFace(tx, py + 6.0'f32, 16.0'f32, hex(0x1D3A6B), SERIF_FONT, val)
      if focused and (int(nowSec * 1000.0'f32) div 530) mod 2 == 0:
        let cx = tx + mTextW(val, 16.0'f32)
        d = d.rect(cx, py + 8.0'f32, 1.5'f32, 16.0'f32, hex(0x1D3A6B))
    elif focused and (int(nowSec * 1000.0'f32) div 530) mod 2 == 0:
      d = d.rect(tx, py + 8.0'f32, 1.5'f32, 16.0'f32, hex(0x1D3A6B))
    d = d.line(rx, py + 27.0'f32, rx + rw, py + 27.0'f32,
               (if focused: hex(0x1D3A6B) else: argb(43, 33, 24, 0.35'f32)), 1.0'f32)

proc drawMoves*(d: var Draw) =
  let baseY = SHY + 75.0'f32
  if gGame.hist.len == 0:
    d = d.tFace(PX + 44.0'f32, baseY, 15.0'f32, hex(0x9A8466),
                   SERIF_FONT, "The sheet is blank \u2014 White opens.")
    return

  const maxVisibleRows = 9
  let totalRows = (gGame.hist.len + 1) div 2
  let startRow = clampI(movesScrollRow, 0, max(0, totalRows - maxVisibleRows))
  let endRow = min(totalRows, startRow + maxVisibleRows)
  let hiCol = argb(242, 196, 90, 0.60'f32)

  for r in startRow ..< endRow:
    let i = r * 2
    let rowY = baseY + float32(r - startRow) * 26.0'f32
    d = d.tFace(PX + 44.0'f32, rowY + 3.0'f32, 13.0'f32, hex(0x8D7555),
                   SERIF_FONT, $(r + 1) & ".")

    let wSan = gGame.hist[i].san
    let wX = PX + 78.0'f32
    if i == gGame.hist.len - 1:
      let ww = max(24.0'f32, mTextW(wSan, 17.0'f32))
      d = d.rect(wX - 3.0'f32, rowY + 12.5'f32, ww + 6.0'f32, 9.8'f32, hiCol, 2.0'f32)
    d = d.tFace(wX, rowY, 17.0'f32, hex(0x1D3A6B), SERIF_FONT, wSan)

    if i + 1 < gGame.hist.len:
      let bSan = gGame.hist[i + 1].san
      let bX = PX + 236.0'f32
      if i + 1 == gGame.hist.len - 1:
        let bw = max(24.0'f32, mTextW(bSan, 17.0'f32))
        d = d.rect(bX - 3.0'f32, rowY + 12.5'f32, bw + 6.0'f32, 9.8'f32, hiCol, 2.0'f32)
      d = d.tFace(bX, rowY, 17.0'f32, hex(0x1D3A6B), SERIF_FONT, bSan)

proc drawResultText*(d: var Draw) =
  if not gGame.ending.isOver: return
  let res = if gGame.ending.kind == "mate":
              (if gGame.ending.winner == 'w': "1\u20130" else: "0\u20131")
            else:
              "\u00bd\u2013\u00bd"
  let rw = mTextW(res, 18.0'f32)
  d = d.tFace(PX + PW - 16.0'f32 - rw, SHY + SHH - 34.0'f32, 18.0'f32,
                 hex(0x8C2A1A), SERIF_FONT, res)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 10. POINTER, KEYBOARD & FRAME LOOP HANDLERS
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

proc hitSquareAt(px, py: float32): int32 =
  if px < BX or px >= BX + BW or py < BY or py >= BY + BW: return -1'i32
  let col = int32((px - BX) / SQP)
  let row = int32((py - BY) / SQP)
  if col < 0 or col > 7 or row < 0 or row > 7: return -1'i32
  fromDisp(row, col)

proc hitButtonAt(px, py: float32): int =
  if py < CTY or py > CTY + CTH + 2.0'f32: return -1
  for b in 0 .. 3:
    let bx = PX + float32(b) * (BTW + BTG)
    if px >= bx and px <= bx + BTW: return b
  -1

proc hitPromoAt(px, py: float32): int =
  if not promoPending.active: return -1
  let (x, y, w, h, fromTop) = promoBoxGeom()
  if px < x or px > x + w or py < y or py > y + h: return -1
  const cancelH = 26.0'f32
  let relY = py - y
  if fromTop:
    if relY >= 4.0'f32 * SQS: return 4
    return clampI(int(relY / SQS), 0, 3)
  else:
    if relY < cancelH: return 0
    return 1 + clampI(int((relY - cancelH) / SQS), 0, 3)

proc resolvePromoIdx(idx: int) =
  let (row, _) = dispRowCol(promoPending.move.dst)
  let fromTop = row == 0
  if fromTop:
    const types = ['Q', 'R', 'B', 'N']
    if idx in 0 .. 3: closePromo(types[idx])
    else: closePromo('\0')
  else:
    const types = ['N', 'B', 'R', 'Q']
    if idx == 0: closePromo('\0')
    elif idx in 1 .. 4: closePromo(types[idx - 1])
    else: closePromo('\0')

proc hitNameInputAt(px, py: float32): int =
  let y0 = SHY + 32.0'f32
  let y1 = SHY + 66.0'f32
  if py < y0 or py > y1: return 0
  if px >= PX + 44.0'f32 and px <= PX + 218.0'f32: return 1
  if px >= PX + 226.0'f32 and px <= PX + PW - 16.0'f32: return 2
  0

proc onPointerDown*() =
  let (px, py) = pointerPos()
  kbFocusVisible = false

  let inp = hitNameInputAt(px, py)
  if inp != activeInput:
    activeInput = inp
    invalidateScene()
  if inp != 0:
    repaint()
    return

  if promoPending.active:
    let pIdx = hitPromoAt(px, py)
    if pIdx >= 0:
      resolvePromoIdx(pIdx)
    else:
      closePromo('\0')
    repaint()
    return

  let btn = hitButtonAt(px, py)
  if btn >= 0:
    if btn == 0 and gGame.hist.len == 0: return
    pressBtn = btn
    hovBtn = btn
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()
    return

  let s = hitSquareAt(px, py)
  if s < 0:
    if selSq >= 0:
      selSq = -1
      invalidateScene()
      repaint()
    return

  focusSq = s
  let p = gGame.st.board[s]
  if selSq >= 0 and s != selSq:
    var isLegalTarget = false
    for m in gGame.legalMoves:
      if m.src == selSq and m.dst == s:
        isLegalTarget = true
        break
    if isLegalTarget:
      discard tryMove(selSq, s, false)
      repaint()
      return

  if (not p.isEmpty) and p.c == gGame.st.turn and (not gGame.ending.isOver):
    let wasSel = (selSq == s)
    selSq = s
    dragArmed = true
    dragActive = false
    dragSq = s
    dragPiece = p
    dragWasSelected = wasSel
    dragStartX = px
    dragStartY = py
    dragCurX = px
    dragCurY = py
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()
    return

  if selSq >= 0:
    selSq = -1
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()

proc onPointerMove*() =
  let (px, py) = pointerPos()
  var dirty = false

  if promoPending.active:
    let pIdx = hitPromoAt(px, py)
    if pIdx != promoPending.hoverIdx:
      promoPending.hoverIdx = pIdx
      dirty = true

  if dragArmed:
    let dist = sqrt((px - dragStartX) * (px - dragStartX) + (py - dragStartY) * (py - dragStartY))
    if (not dragActive) and dist > 5.0'f32:
      dragActive = true
    if dragActive:
      dragCurX = px
      dragCurY = py
      dirty = true

  let b = hitButtonAt(px, py)
  if b != hovBtn:
    hovBtn = b
    dirty = true

  let sq = hitSquareAt(px, py)
  if sq != hoverSq:
    hoverSq = sq
    if selSq >= 0: dirty = true

  if dirty:
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()

proc onPointerUp*() =
  let (px, py) = pointerPos()

  if pressBtn >= 0:
    let b = hitButtonAt(px, py)
    let triggered = (b == pressBtn)
    let which = pressBtn
    pressBtn = -1
    invalidateScene()
    if triggered:
      case which
      of 0: doUndo()
      of 1: doFlip()
      of 2: doNewGame()
      of 3:
        soundOn = not soundOn
        if soundOn: soundTick()
        invalidateScene()
        repaint()
      else: discard
    else:
      repaint()
    return

  if dragArmed:
    let wasActive = dragActive
    let origSq = dragSq
    let wasSel = dragWasSelected
    dragArmed = false
    dragActive = false
    dragSq = -1
    if wasActive:
      let dst = hitSquareAt(px, py)
      if dst >= 0 and dst != origSq and tryMove(origSq, dst, true):
        repaint()
        return
      let (tx, ty) = sqScreenPos(origSq)
      gliders.add Glider(
        piece: dragPiece,
        x0: px - PCSZ * 0.5'f32, y0: py - PCSZ * 0.5'f32,
        x1: tx, y1: ty,
        t0: nowSec, dur: 0.16'f32, dropped: true)
      invalidateScene()
      repaint()
      return
    else:
      if wasSel:
        selSq = -1
      else:
        soundTick()
      invalidateScene()
      repaint()

proc activateSquare(s: int32) =
  if promoPending.active or s < 0 or s > 63: return
  let p = gGame.st.board[s]
  if selSq >= 0 and s != selSq:
    for m in gGame.legalMoves:
      if m.src == selSq and m.dst == s:
        discard tryMove(selSq, s, false)
        repaint()
        return
  if (not p.isEmpty) and p.c == gGame.st.turn and (not gGame.ending.isOver):
    if selSq == s:
      selSq = -1
    else:
      selSq = s
      soundTick()
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()
    return
  if selSq >= 0:
    selSq = -1
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()

proc onKey*() =
  let k = lastKey()

  if activeInput != 0:
    if k == keyEscape or k == keyEnter:
      activeInput = 0
      handled()
      invalidateScene()
      repaint()
      return
    if k == keyTab:
      activeInput = if activeInput == 1: 2 else: 1
      handled()
      invalidateScene()
      repaint()
      return
    if k == keyBackspace:
      if activeInput == 1 and nameWStr.len > 0:
        nameWStr.setLen(nameWStr.len - 1)
      elif activeInput == 2 and nameBStr.len > 0:
        nameBStr.setLen(nameBStr.len - 1)
      handled()
      invalidateScene()
      repaint()
      return
    if (not k.ctrl) and (not k.alt) and k.code >= 32 and k.code < 127:
      var ch = char(k.code)
      if ch in {'a'..'z'} and k.shift:
        ch = char(ord(ch) - 32)
      if activeInput == 1 and nameWStr.len < 24:
        nameWStr.add ch
      elif activeInput == 2 and nameBStr.len < 24:
        nameBStr.add ch
      handled()
      invalidateScene()
      repaint()
      return
    return

  if promoPending.active:
    if k == keyEscape:
      closePromo('\0')
      handled()
      repaint()
      return
    if k == keyDown or k == keyUp:
      let delta = if k == keyDown: 1 else: -1
      promoPending.focusIdx = (promoPending.focusIdx + delta + 5) mod 5
      handled()
      invalidateScene()
      repaint()
      return
    if k == keyEnter or k == keySpace:
      resolvePromoIdx(promoPending.focusIdx)
      handled()
      repaint()
      return
    return

  if k.ctrl or k.alt: return

  if k == 'u':
    doUndo()
    handled()
    return
  if k == 'f':
    doFlip()
    handled()
    return

  var (row, col) = dispRowCol(focusSq)
  if k == keyUp:
    row = max(0'i32, row - 1'i32)
    kbFocusVisible = true
    focusSq = fromDisp(row, col)
    handled()
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()
  elif k == keyDown:
    row = min(7'i32, row + 1'i32)
    kbFocusVisible = true
    focusSq = fromDisp(row, col)
    handled()
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()
  elif k == keyLeft:
    col = max(0'i32, col - 1'i32)
    kbFocusVisible = true
    focusSq = fromDisp(row, col)
    handled()
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()
  elif k == keyRight:
    col = min(7'i32, col + 1'i32)
    kbFocusVisible = true
    focusSq = fromDisp(row, col)
    handled()
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()
  elif k == keyEnter or k == keySpace:
    kbFocusVisible = true
    activateSquare(focusSq)
    handled()
  elif k == keyEscape:
    if selSq >= 0 or kbFocusVisible:
      selSq = -1
      kbFocusVisible = false
      handled()
      invalidateScene()
      repaint()

proc tick*() =
  let dt = cl(frameDt(), 0.0'f32, 0.10'f32)
  nowSec += dt
  var dirty = false

  if stepAudio():
    discard

  if confirmT0 >= 0.0'f32 and (nowSec - confirmT0) > 2.6'f32:
    confirmT0 = -1.0'f32
    dirty = true

  if flipT0 >= 0.0'f32:
    let elapsed = nowSec - flipT0
    if (not flipSwapped) and elapsed >= 0.19'f32:
      gFlip = not gFlip
      flipSwapped = true
    if elapsed >= 0.37'f32:
      flipT0 = -1.0'f32
    dirty = true

  if appearT0 >= 0.0'f32:
    if (nowSec - appearT0) <= 1.25'f32:
      dirty = true
    else:
      appearT0 = -10.0'f32
      dirty = true

  if gliders.len > 0:
    var i = 0
    while i < gliders.len:
      if (nowSec - gliders[i].t0) >= gliders[i].dur:
        gliders.delete(i)
      else:
        inc i
    dirty = true

  if fadingPieces.len > 0:
    var i = 0
    while i < fadingPieces.len:
      if (nowSec - fadingPieces[i].t0) >= 0.36'f32:
        fadingPieces.delete(i)
      else:
        inc i
    dirty = true

  if activeInput != 0:
    dirty = true

  if dirty:
    invalidateScene()
    repaint()

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 11. STATIC & COMPOSITE SCENE BUILDERS
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

proc drawFrame(d: var Draw) =
  d = d.layer(frameLayer, FX, FY, B, B)
  let rim = argb(0, 0, 0, 0.60'f32)
  let rr = FR * 0.32'f32
  d.cmds.add "B " & fmtNum(FX + 0.5'f32) & " " & fmtNum(FY + 0.5'f32) & " " &
             fmtNum(B - 1.0'f32) & " " & fmtNum(B - 1.0'f32) & " " &
             fmtNum(rr) & " " & $rim & " 1.00;"
  d = d.rect(FX, FY, B, 1.0'f32, argb(255, 230, 200, 0.22'f32))
  let gi = 16.5'f32
  let gh = argb(201, 164, 92, 0.5'f32)
  let gd = argb(0, 0, 0, 0.35'f32)
  d = d.line(FX + gi - 1.0'f32, FY + gi - 1.0'f32, FX + B - gi + 1.0'f32, FY + gi - 1.0'f32, gd, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.line(FX + gi - 1.0'f32, FY + B - gi + 1.0'f32, FX + B - gi + 1.0'f32, FY + B - gi + 1.0'f32, gd, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.line(FX + gi - 1.0'f32, FY + gi - 1.0'f32, FX + gi - 1.0'f32, FY + B - gi + 1.0'f32, gd, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.line(FX + B - gi + 1.0'f32, FY + gi - 1.0'f32, FX + B - gi + 1.0'f32, FY + B - gi + 1.0'f32, gd, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.line(FX + gi, FY + gi, FX + B - gi, FY + gi, gh, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.line(FX + gi, FY + B - gi, FX + B - gi, FY + B - gi, gh, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.line(FX + gi, FY + gi, FX + gi, FY + B - gi, gh, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.line(FX + B - gi, FY + gi, FX + B - gi, FY + B - gi, gh, 1.0'f32)

proc drawCorner(d: var Draw; cx, cy: float32; flipX, flipY: bool) =
  var idx = 0
  if flipX: idx += 1
  if flipY: idx += 2
  let x0 = (if flipX: cx - CBOX else: cx)
  let y0 = (if flipY: cy - CBOX else: cy)
  d = d.layer(cornerL[idx], x0, y0, CBOX, CBOX)

proc drawCoords(d: var Draw) =
  const LH  = 0.7175'f32
  const CDX = 2.0'f32
  let size = FR * 0.42'f32
  let files = if gFlip: ['h', 'g', 'f', 'e', 'd', 'c', 'b', 'a']
              else:     ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'e', 'f', 'g', 'h']
  let dim = argb(217, 183, 108, 0.55'f32)
  let lit = C_COORD
  for k in 0 ..< 8:
    let ccx = BX + (float32(k) + 0.5'f32) * SQP
    let ccy = BY + (float32(k) + 0.5'f32) * SQP
    let rk = if gFlip: $chr(ord('1') + k) else: $chr(ord('8') - k)
    centred(d, ccx + CDX, FY + FR * 0.5'f32 - size * LH, size, dim, $files[k], size * 0.04'f32)
    centred(d, ccx + CDX, FY + B - FR * 0.5'f32 - size * LH, size, lit, $files[k], size * 0.04'f32)
    centred(d, FX + FR * 0.5'f32 + CDX, ccy - size * LH, size, lit, rk, size * 0.04'f32)
    centred(d, FX + B - FR * 0.5'f32 + CDX, ccy - size * LH, size, dim, rk, size * 0.04'f32)

proc drawTitle(d: var Draw) =
  spaced(d, PX, 28.0'f32, 12.0'f32, C_BRASS, "A HOT-SEAT GAME FOR TWO", 12.0'f32 * 0.34'f32)
  d = d.tFace(PX, 45.0'f32, 34.56'f32, hex(0xF6ECD6), SERIF_FONT, "Grandmaster's Study")
  let ruleY = 105.5'f32
  let rw = (PW - 14.0'f32 - 20.0'f32) * 0.5'f32
  for i in 0 ..< int(rw):
    let u = float32(i) / rw
    let a = 0.55'f32 * (1.0'f32 - abs(2.0'f32 * u - 1.0'f32))
    d = d.line(PX + float32(i), ruleY, PX + float32(i) + 1.0'f32, ruleY,
               argb(201, 164, 92, a), 1.0'f32)
    d = d.line(PX + PW - float32(i) - 1.0'f32, ruleY, PX + PW - float32(i), ruleY,
               argb(201, 164, 92, a), 1.0'f32)
  d = d.svgPath("M7 1l1.8 4.2L13 7l-4.2 1.8L7 13l-1.8-4.2L1 7l4.2-1.8Z",
                PX + PW * 0.5'f32 - 7.0'f32, ruleY - 6.0'f32, 1.0'f32, C_BRASS)

proc drawStatus(d: var Draw) =
  let mode = statusMode()
  let plate = if mode == 1: argb(60, 10, 6, 0.5'f32)
              elif mode == 2: argb(40, 28, 10, 0.55'f32)
              else: argb(10, 16, 13, 0.55'f32)
  d = d.rect(PX, STY, PW, STH, plate, 10.0'f32)
  let hb = if mode == 1: argb(230, 70, 50, 0.55'f32)
           elif mode == 2: argb(201, 164, 92, 0.6'f32)
           else: argb(201, 164, 92, 0.22'f32)
  d = d.line(PX + 10.0'f32, STY + 0.5'f32, PX + PW - 10.0'f32, STY + 0.5'f32, hb, 1.0'f32)
  d = d.line(PX + 10.0'f32, STY + STH - 0.5'f32, PX + PW - 10.0'f32, STY + STH - 0.5'f32, hb, 1.0'f32)
  let dcx = PX + 16.0'f32 + 9.0'f32
  let dcy = STY + STH * 0.5'f32
  let black = sideToMove() == 'b'
  d = d.circle(dcx, dcy, 10.0'f32, (if black: hex(0x7A5C2E) else: hex(0x6B5638)))
  d = d.circle(dcx, dcy, 9.0'f32, (if black: hex(0x1A140E) else: hex(0xEFE6CC)))
  let stcol = if mode == 1: hex(0xFFB9A6) elif mode == 2: hex(0xF3D58D) else: C_IVORY
  d = d.tFace(PX + 16.0'f32 + 18.0'f32 + 12.0'f32, STY + 13.0'f32, 19.0'f32, stcol,
                 SERIF_FONT, statusText())

proc drawButton(d: var Draw; x, y: float32; icon: seq[string]; label: string; b: int) =
  let dim = b == 0 and gGame.hist.len == 0
  let hov = b == hovBtn and not dim
  let pressed = b == pressBtn and b == hovBtn and not dim
  let confirm = b == 2 and confirmT0 >= 0.0'f32
  let muted = b == 3 and not soundOn
  let lab = if confirm: "Sure?" else: label
  let yy = y + (if pressed: 2.0'f32 else: 0.0'f32)
  if not pressed:
    d = d.rect(x, y, BTW, CTH + 2.0'f32, (if dim: desaturate(hex(0x5B4320)) else: hex(0x5B4320)), 8.0'f32)
    d = d.rect(x + 5.0'f32, y + 19.0'f32, BTW - 10.0'f32, 24.0'f32, argb(0, 0, 0, 0.30'f32), 12.0'f32)
  else:
    d = d.rect(x + 5.0'f32, y + 21.0'f32, BTW - 10.0'f32, 18.0'f32, argb(0, 0, 0, 0.25'f32), 9.0'f32)
  let plate = if confirm: confirmL elif muted: muteL elif dim: btnDimLayer else: btnLayer
  d = d.layer(plate, x, yy, BTW, CTH)
  if hov and not pressed:
    d = d.rect(x, yy, BTW, CTH, argb(255, 255, 255, 0.065'f32), 8.0'f32)
  var ink = if confirm: hex(0x2A0C05)
            elif dim: desaturate(hex(0x2C1C0B)) else: hex(0x2C1C0B)
  if muted: ink = satBriC(ink, 0.35'f32, 0.86'f32)
  elif hov and not pressed: ink = brightC(ink, 1.07'f32)
  for i, p in icon:
    var ic = ink
    if muted and b == 3 and i > 0:
      ic = (ic and 0x00FFFFFF'u32) or 0x33000000'u32
    d = d.svgPath(p, x + BTW * 0.5'f32 - 9.0'f32, yy + 11.25'f32, 1.0'f32, ic, 1.6'f32)
  let lw = float32(lab.len) * (11.5'f32 * 0.667'f32 + 11.5'f32 * 0.14'f32)
  spaced(d, x + BTW * 0.5'f32 - lw * 0.5'f32, yy + 33.25'f32, 11.5'f32, ink, lab,
         11.5'f32 * 0.14'f32)

proc drawControls(d: var Draw) =
  drawButton(d, PX, CTY, @["M6.5 4 3 7.5 6.5 11", "M3.5 7.5h7a4 4 0 0 1 0 8H8"], "Undo", 0)
  drawButton(d, PX + BTW + BTG, CTY,
             @["M5 2.5v12M2.5 12 5 14.5 7.5 12M13 15.5v-12M10.5 6 13 3.5 15.5 6"], "Flip", 1)
  drawButton(d, PX + 2.0'f32 * (BTW + BTG), CTY, @["M9 3v12M3 9h12"], "New", 2)
  drawButton(d, PX + 3.0'f32 * (BTW + BTG), CTY,
             @["M3 7h3l4-3v10l-4-3H3Z",
              "M12.5 6.5c1 1.4 1 3.6 0 5M14.5 4.5c2.2 2.6 2.2 6.4 0 9"], "Sound", 3)

proc drawTray(d: var Draw) =
  d = d.layer(trayLayer, PX, TRY, float32(TYW), float32(TYH))
  d = d.line(PX + 1.0'f32, TRY + 41.5'f32, PX + PW - 1.0'f32, TRY + 41.5'f32,
             argb(255, 255, 255, 0.06'f32), 1.0'f32)
  let who = argb(232, 207, 151, 0.75'f32)
  let dash = argb(232, 207, 151, 0.30'f32)
  let (byW, byB, advW, advB) = trayLists()
  for r in 0 .. 1:
    let ty = TRY + 11.0'f32 + float32(r) * 41.0'f32
    spaced(d, PX + 15.0'f32, ty, 11.0'f32, who,
           (if r == 0: "WHITE TOOK" else: "BLACK TOOK"), 11.0'f32 * 0.18'f32)
    let caps = if r == 0: byW else: byB
    if caps.len == 0:
      d = d.tFace(PX + 117.0'f32, ty + 6.0'f32, 16.0'f32, dash, SERIF_FONT, "\u2014")
    else:
      drawCapsRow(d, r, caps, (if r == 0: 'b' else: 'w'))
    let adv = if r == 0: advW else: advB
    if adv > 0:
      let at = "+" & $adv
      let aw = mTextW(at, 14.0'f32)
      d = d.tFace(PX + PW - 16.0'f32 - aw, ty + 4.0'f32, 14.0'f32, hex(0xE8CF97),
                     SERIF_FONT, at)

proc drawSheet(d: var Draw) =
  d = d.layer(sheetLayer, PX, SHY, PW, SHH)
  let rule = argb(70, 110, 150, 0.18'f32)
  var y = SHY + 25.0'f32
  while y < SHY + SHH - 1.0'f32:
    d = d.line(PX + 1.0'f32, y + 0.5'f32, PX + PW - 1.0'f32, y + 0.5'f32, rule, 1.0'f32)
    y += 26.0'f32
  d = d.line(PX + 30.0'f32, SHY + 1.0'f32, PX + 30.0'f32, SHY + SHH - 1.0'f32,
             argb(190, 60, 50, 0.45'f32), 1.0'f32)
  spaced(d, PX + 44.0'f32, SHY + 14.0'f32, 13.0'f32, hex(0x6D5335), "SCORE SHEET",
         13.0'f32 * 0.3'f32)
  let gstr = "Game " & $gNumber
  let smallW = float32(gstr.len) * (11.0'f32 * 0.667'f32 + 11.0'f32 * 0.12'f32)
  spaced(d, PX + PW - 16.0'f32 - smallW, SHY + 14.0'f32, 11.0'f32, hex(0x9A8466),
         gstr, 11.0'f32 * 0.12'f32)
  let py = SHY + 35.0'f32
  spaced(d, PX + 44.0'f32, py + 7.0'f32, 11.0'f32, hex(0x7A6346), "WHITE", 11.0'f32 * 0.16'f32)
  spaced(d, PX + 226.0'f32, py + 7.0'f32, 11.0'f32, hex(0x7A6346), "BLACK", 11.0'f32 * 0.16'f32)
  drawNames(d)
  drawMoves(d)
  drawResultText(d)
  d = d.tFace(PX + PW * 0.5'f32 - 110.0'f32, SHY + SHH + 15.0'f32, 12.5'f32,
                 argb(232, 207, 151, 0.60'f32), SERIF_FONT,
                 "Click or drag a piece \u00b7 U undo \u00b7 F flip")

proc buildScene(): Draw =
  result = Draw()
  result = result.layer(bgLayer, 0, 0, SW, SH)
  let hb = argb(201, 164, 92, 0.24'f32)
  result = result.line(12.0'f32, 12.5'f32, SW - 12.0'f32, 12.5'f32, hb, 1.0'f32)
  result = result.line(12.0'f32, SH - 12.5'f32, SW - 12.0'f32, SH - 12.5'f32, hb, 1.0'f32)
  result = result.line(12.5'f32, 12.0'f32, 12.5'f32, SH - 12.0'f32, hb, 1.0'f32)
  result = result.line(SW - 12.5'f32, 12.0'f32, SW - 12.5'f32, SH - 12.0'f32, hb, 1.0'f32)
  let wash = argb(201, 164, 92, 0.10'f32)
  result = result.rect(12.0'f32, 13.0'f32, SW - 24.0'f32, 5.0'f32, wash)
  result = result.rect(12.0'f32, SH - 18.0'f32, SW - 24.0'f32, 5.0'f32, wash)
  result = result.rect(13.0'f32, 13.0'f32, 5.0'f32, SH - 26.0'f32, wash)
  result = result.rect(SW - 18.0'f32, 13.0'f32, 5.0'f32, SH - 26.0'f32, wash)

  drawFrame(result)
  drawCorner(result, FX - 3.0'f32, FY - 3.0'f32, false, false)
  drawCorner(result, FX + B + 3.0'f32, FY - 3.0'f32, true, false)
  drawCorner(result, FX - 3.0'f32, FY + B + 3.0'f32, false, true)
  drawCorner(result, FX + B + 3.0'f32, FY + B + 3.0'f32, true, true)
  drawCoords(result)

  let fk = fadeK()
  if fk < 1.0'f32 and fk > 0.001'f32:
    result = result.alpha(fk)
  if fk > 0.001'f32:
    result = result.rect(BX - 5.0'f32, BY - 5.0'f32, BW + 10.0'f32, BW + 10.0'f32, hex(0x4A3418))
    result = result.rect(BX - 4.0'f32, BY - 4.0'f32, BW + 8.0'f32, BW + 8.0'f32, hex(0xB8914A))
    result = result.rect(BX - 2.0'f32, BY - 2.0'f32, BW + 4.0'f32, BW + 4.0'f32, hex(0x190704))
    let bLayer = if gFlip: boardFlipLayer else: boardLayer
    result = result.layer(bLayer, BX, BY, BW, BW)
    drawHi(result)
    drawPiecesAnim(result)
    drawGliders(result)
    drawPromo(result)
  if fk < 1.0'f32 and fk > 0.001'f32:
    result = result.alpha(1.0'f32 / fk)

  drawTitle(result)
  drawStatus(result)
  drawControls(result)
  drawTray(result)
  drawSheet(result)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# 12. ENTRY POINT (`yantra_main`)
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

proc repaint*() =
  if not ready: return
  if sceneCmds.len == 0:
    sceneCmds = buildScene().cmds
  scene.set(pDraw, sceneCmds)

proc main() =
  page = window(title = "Grandmaster's Study", w = int32(SW), h = int32(SH))
  scene = canvas(page, int32(SW), int32(SH))

  gGame = newGame()
  bakeBg()
  bakeFrame()
  bakeBoard()
  bakeButton()
  bakeTray()
  bakeSheet()
  bakePieceShadows()
  bakePieces()
  bakeCorners()
  bakeExtra()
  loadSounds()
  syncPieces()
  refreshLegal()

  scene.addHandler(evFrame, proc() = tick())
  scene.addHandler(evPointerDown, proc() = onPointerDown())
  scene.addHandler(evPointerMove, proc() = onPointerMove())
  scene.addHandler(evPointerUp, proc() = onPointerUp())
  page.addHandler(evKey, proc() = onKey())
  appearT0 = -10.0'f32
  ready = true
  repaint()

proc yantra_main*() {.exportc: "yantra_main".} = main()


================================================================
FILE: apps/chess/engine.nim
